### [1] [Superficial Success vs. Internal Breakdown: An Empirical Study of Generalization in Adaptive Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18951)
*Namyoung So,Seokgyu Jang,Taeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究揭示了自适应多智能体系统存在“拓扑过拟合”和“虚假协调”两大关键缺陷，指出其泛化能力不足，呼吁开发应优先考虑泛化性并采用更深入、多维度的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应多智能体系统（MAS）被广泛用于解决复杂问题，但其优化往往局限于狭窄的任务范围。现有研究尚不清楚此类系统能否作为通用系统在不同领域间有效泛化并保持理想的行为模式，这引发了对其实用性的担忧。

Method: 对自适应多智能体系统进行了广泛且深入的实证研究（extensive empirical study），分析其在不同领域的表现及内部智能体交互行为。

Result: 发现了两个关键问题：(1) 拓扑过拟合（topological overfitting）：系统无法在不同领域间泛化；(2) 虚假协调（illusory coordination）：系统虽然在表面准确率上表现尚可，但底层的智能体交互模式偏离了理想的MAS行为。

Conclusion: 当前自适应MAS的泛化能力存在严重不足，仅在最终答案正确性层面进行评估具有误导性。迫切需要在MAS开发中优先考虑泛化性，并建立超越简单结果正确性、能够评估底层交互行为的评估协议。

Abstract: Adaptive multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly adopted to tackle complex problems.However, the narrow task coverage of their optimization raises the question of whether they can function as general-purpose systems.To address this gap, we conduct an extensive empirical study of adaptive MAS, revealing two key findings: (1) topological overfitting -- they fail to generalize across different domains; and (2) illusory coordination -- they achieve reasonable surface-level accuracy while the underlying agent interactions diverge from ideal MAS behavior, raising concerns about their practical utility.These findings highlight the pressing need to prioritize generalization in MAS development and motivate evaluation protocols that extend beyond simple final-answer correctness.

</details>


### [2] [Gated Coordination for Efficient Multi-Agent Collaboration in Minecraft Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18975)
*HuaDong Jian,Chenghao Li,Haoyu Wang,Jiajia Shuai,Jinyu Guo,Yang Yang,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分区信息架构和门控升级机制，将多智能体系统中的通信从默认反应转变为选择性决策，显著提升了长周期开放世界任务中的协调效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长周期开放世界多智能体系统将局部异常自动触发通信作为默认设计，导致协调噪声、中断本地执行以及过度使用公共交互，许多本可本地解决的案例却引发了不必要的通信。

Method: 提出分区信息架构，显式分离私有执行状态和公共协调状态；引入两种机制：1) 基于系统验证结果的事件触发工作记忆，维持紧凑低噪声的局部状态表示；2) 成本敏感的门控升级机制，通过联合考虑节点关键性、本地恢复成本和下游任务影响来决定是否启动跨区域通信。

Result: 在开放环境下的长周期建造任务实验中，相较于基于强通信和规划结构的基线模型，该方法在蓝图完成质量和执行链长度方面表现更优，同时提升了本地自恢复能力，减少了无效升级，并提高了公共通信的效用。

Conclusion: 通过引入门控通信和分区信息架构，成功将通信从默认反应转变为选择性决策，在保持高性能的同时显著改善了多智能体系统的协调效率。

Abstract: In long-horizon open-world multi-agent systems, existing methods often treat local anomalies as automatic triggers for communication. This default design introduces coordination noise, interrupts local execution, and overuses public interaction in cases that could be resolved locally. To address this issue, we propose a partitioned information architecture for MLLM agents that explicitly separates private execution states from public coordination states. Building on this design, we introduce two key mechanisms. First, we develop an event-triggered working memory based on system-verified outcomes to maintain compact and low-noise local state representations. Second, we propose a cost-sensitive gated escalation mechanism that determines whether cross-region communication should be initiated by jointly considering node criticality, local recovery cost, and downstream task impact. In this way, communication is transformed from a default reaction into a selective decision. Experiments conducted on long-term construction tasks in open environments demonstrate that, compared to baseline models based on strong communication and planned structures, the introduction of gated communication and a partitioned information architecture results in superior performance in terms of blueprint completion quality and execution chain length. It also improves local self-recovery, reduces ineffective escalations, and increases the utility of public communication.

</details>


### [3] [ClawCoin: An Agentic AI-Native Cryptocurrency for Decentralized Agent Economies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19026)
*Shaoyu Li,Chaoyu Zhang,Hexuan Yu,Y. Thomas Hou,Wenjing Lou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了ClawCoin，一种面向去中心化智能体经济的、以计算成本为指数的代币化记账单位和结算资产，旨在解决现有API计算代币不可转移、不可链上结算的问题，从而改善智能体间的协调与合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主AI智能体的运行依赖于API计算代币，但该资源具有账户绑定、供应商特定且不出现在链上账本等特性，导致其不可转移。现有的支付协议（如x402）仅能在智能体间转移法币价值，无法代表智能体实际消耗的计算成本，导致智能体无法以与计算成本对齐的单位进行报价、托管或结算工作流。

Method: ClawCoin结合了四个层面：1) 基于标准化价格的稳健篮子指数；2) 发布签名新鲜认证的预言机；3) 具有覆盖率阈值和速率限制的基于资产净值(NAV)的铸造/赎回金库；4) 用于多跳委托的链上结算层。作者在兼容以太坊的L2上实现了原型，并使用多智能体模拟器和OpenClaw测试平台进行了评估。

Result: 在单智能体、多智能体、工作流和采购实验中，ClawCoin能够在成本冲击下稳定执行能力，减少跨智能体报价离散度，消除部分结算，并维持合作市场动态，而以法币计价的基线方法无法实现这些效果。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，以计算成本为指数的记账单位可以改善去中心化智能体间的协调，为新兴的智能体经济提供了一种有效的计算资源结算方案。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents live or die by the API tokens they consume: without paid inference capacity they cannot reason, act, or delegate. Compute-token cost has become the binding resource of the emerging agent economy, yet it is non-transferable: it is account-bound, vendor-specific, and absent from on-chain ledgers. Existing payment rails such as x402 move fiat-backed value between agents, but they do not represent the quantity agents actually burn. As a result, agents can transport purchasing power but cannot quote, escrow, or settle workflows in a unit aligned with compute cost.
  We present ClawCoin, a tokenized, compute-cost-indexed unit of account and settlement asset for decentralized agent economies. ClawCoin combines four layers: a robust basket index over standardized prices; an oracle publishing signed fresh attestations; a NAV-based mint/redeem vault with coverage thresholds and rate limits; and an on-chain settlement layer for multi-hop delegations.
  We implement a prototype on an Ethereum-compatible L2 and evaluate it using a multi-agent simulator and the OpenClaw testbed. Across single-agent, multi-agent, workflow, and procurement experiments, ClawCoin stabilizes execution capacity under cost shocks, reduces cross-agent quote dispersion, eliminates partial settlements, and sustains cooperative market dynamics that fiat-denominated baselines cannot. These results suggest that compute-indexed units of account can improve decentralized agent coordination.

</details>


### [4] [Mesh Memory Protocol: Semantic Infrastructure for Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19540)
*Hongwei Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出了Mesh Memory Protocol (MMP)，一种支持跨会话LLM智能体认知协作的语义基础设施协议，通过字段级评估、可追溯性和角色相关存储，解决了长期协作场景下的认知状态共享问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体团队协作任务周期延长至数天或数周，智能体需要跨会话实时共享、评估和融合彼此的认知状态。现有协议无法满足三个核心需求：字段级选择性接受、声明可追溯性、以及基于存储方式的会话重启后记忆相关性，因此需要新的语义层协议。

Method: 提出Mesh Memory Protocol (MMP)作为语义基础设施，包含四个组合原语：(1) CAT7：固定七字段认知记忆块模式；(2) SVAF：基于接收者角色锚点的字段级评估机制；(3) 智能体间谱系：通过内容哈希键的父母和祖先追踪实现声明溯源；(4) 混合机制：仅存储接收者角色评估后的理解而非原始信号。

Result: MMP已规范定义、发布并在三个参考部署中生产运行。每个会话作为网格对等节点运行自主智能体，具有独立身份和记忆，与其他智能体跨网络协作实现集体智能。

Conclusion: MMP填补了智能体通信语义层的协议空白，与传统工具访问和任务委托协议形成互补。该协议成功实现了跨会话认知协作的基础设施支持，为长期运行的智能体团队协作提供了可验证的解决方案。

Abstract: Teams of LLM agents increasingly collaborate on tasks spanning days or weeks: multi-day data-generation sprints where generator, reviewer, and auditor agents coordinate in real time on overlapping batches; specialists carrying findings forward across session restarts; product decisions compounding over many review rounds. This requires agents to share, evaluate, and combine each other's cognitive state in real time across sessions. We call this cross-session agent-to-agent cognitive collaboration, distinct from parallel agent execution. To enable it, three problems must be solved together. (P1) Each agent decides field by field what to accept from peers, not accept or reject whole messages. (P2) Every claim is traceable to source, so returning claims are recognised as echoes of the receiver's own prior thinking. (P3) Memory that survives session restarts is relevant because of how it was stored, not how it is retrieved. These are protocol-level properties at the semantic layer of agent communication, distinct from tool-access and task-delegation protocols at lower layers. We call this missing protocol layer "semantic infrastructure," and the Mesh Memory Protocol (MMP) specifies it. Four composable primitives work together: CAT7, a fixed seven-field schema for every Cognitive Memory Block (CMB); SVAF, which evaluates each field against the receiver's role-indexed anchors and realises P1; inter-agent lineage, carried as parents and ancestors of content-hash keys and realising P2; and remix, which stores only the receiver's own role-evaluated understanding of each accepted CMB, never the raw peer signal, realising P3. MMP is specified, shipped, and running in production across three reference deployments, where each session runs an autonomous agent as a mesh peer with its own identity and memory, collaborating with other agents across the network for collective intelligence.

</details>


### [5] [FOCAL: Filtered On-device Continuous Activity Logging for Efficient Personal Desktop Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19541)
*Haoran Yin,Zhiyuan Wen,Jiannong Cao,Bo Yuan,Ruosong Yang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出FOCAL，一种隐私优先的多智能体系统，通过级联架构（过滤-规划-记录）在本地将桌面交互流转换为任务组织的个人日志，在大幅降低计算开销的同时显著提升了任务中断场景下的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将桌面交互流转换为任务组织的个人日志面临两大挑战：1) 穷尽的视觉-语言模型(VLM)处理会消耗大量本地资源；2) 全局流处理会导致跨任务上下文污染。

Method: 提出FOCAL系统，采用统一的过滤-规划-记录架构，级联四个智能体：轻量级Filter Agent用于噪声抑制；纯文本Brain Agent用于任务归因；Record Agent用于选择性视觉推理；任务隔离的Memory Agent用于上下文连贯的摘要生成。

Result: 在DesktopBench（420个复杂会话、2,572张截图）上的实验表明：相比基线，FOCAL总token消耗减少60.4%，VLM调用次数减少72.3%，关键信息召回率(KIR)从0.38提升至0.61。在A→B→A任务中断场景下，FOCAL保持Task Acc 0.81和KIR 0.80，而基线Task Acc骤降至0.03。

Conclusion: FOCAL开创了在设备端高效地将无指令桌面流摘要化为多视角个人日志的方法，实现了隐私保护与计算效率的平衡。

Abstract: Desktop interaction streams provide a continuous, privacy-sensitive record of interleaved user tasks. Transforming these streams into task-organized personal logs on-device faces two main challenges: exhaustive Vision-Language Model (VLM) processing strains local resources, and global stream processing causes cross-task context pollution. We present FOCAL (Filtered On-device Continuous Activity Logging), a privacy-first multi-agent system utilizing a unified filter-plan-log architecture. It cascades a lightweight Filter Agent for noise suppression, a text-only Brain Agent for task attribution, a Record Agent for selective visual reasoning, and a task-isolated Memory Agent for context-coherent summarization. Experiments on DesktopBench (comprising 2,572 screenshots across 420 complex sessions) show FOCAL reduces total token consumption by 60.4% and VLM call count by 72.3% versus a baseline, while boosting Key Information Recall (KIR) from 0.38 to 0.61. Crucially, under $A{\to}B{\to}A$ task interruptions, FOCAL maintains Task Acc 0.81 and KIR 0.80, whereas the baseline collapses to Task Acc 0.03. FOCAL pioneers the efficient, on-device summarization of instruction-free desktop streams into multi-perspective personal logs.

</details>


### [6] [TeamFusion: Supporting Open-ended Teamwork with Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19589)
*Jiale Liu,Victor S. Bursztyn,Lin Ai,Haoliang Wang,Sunav Choudhary,Saayan Mitra,Qingyun Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了TeamFusion，一种用于开放领域团队协作的多智能体系统，通过结构化讨论和迭代优化来解决观点聚合问题，优于直接聚合基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放领域中，团队需要协调不同观点以产出高质量成果。然而，现有的常用于封闭领域的答案聚合方法并不适用，因为它们倾向于压制少数派观点，而非解决潜在的分歧。

Method: 提出了TeamFusion多智能体系统，包含三个步骤：1. 根据成员表达偏好实例化代理智能体；2. 进行结构化讨论以揭示共识与分歧；3. 综合生成更具共识导向的成果，并反馈至新的讨论与优化迭代中。

Result: 在两个团队协作任务上的评估显示，TeamFusion在各项指标、任务和团队配置下均优于直接聚合基线。成员能够评估个人观点在团队决策中的代表性及最终成果的共识强度。

Conclusion: TeamFusion有效解决了开放领域团队协作中的观点融合问题，通过结构化讨论机制平衡了不同观点，提升了团队决策的代表性和共识度，为多智能体协作系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: In open-ended domains, teams must reconcile diverse viewpoints to produce strong deliverables. Answer aggregation approaches commonly used in closed domains are ill-suited to this setting, as they tend to suppress minority perspectives rather than resolve underlying disagreements. We present TeamFusion, a multi-agent system designed to support teamwork in open-ended domains by: 1. Instantiating a proxy agent for each team member conditioned on their expressed preferences; 2. Conducting a structured discussion to surface agreements and disagreements; and 3. Synthesizing more consensus-oriented deliverables that feed into new iterations of discussion and refinement. We evaluate TeamFusion on two teamwork tasks where team members can assess how well their individual views are represented in team decisions and how consensually strong the final deliverables are, finding that it outperforms direct aggregation baselines across metrics, tasks, and team configurations.

</details>

### [7] [HadAgent: Harness-Aware Decentralized Agentic AI Serving with Proof-of-Inference Blockchain Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18614)
*Landy Jimenez,Mariah Weatherspoon,Bingyu Shen,Yi Sheng,Jianming Liu,Boyang Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了HadAgent系统，用证明推理（PoI）共识机制取代传统哈希挖矿，使区块链节点通过执行确定性LLM推理任务获得出块权，将原本浪费的计算资源转化为有价值的AI推理服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作量证明（PoW）区块链共识消耗大量计算资源却不产生有用输出，而大语言模型（LLM）智能体的快速增长对GPU计算提出了前所未有的需求。研究旨在解决这两个问题的矛盾，将区块链挖矿的算力转化为有用的AI推理计算。

Method: 提出HadAgent去中心化智能体AI服务系统：1）设计证明推理（PoI）共识机制，节点通过执行确定性LLM推理任务获得出块权，验证仅需在相同条件下重新执行单次前向传播；2）采用三通道区块体结构（DATA、MODEL、PROOF），各通道由独立Merkle根保护实现细粒度篡改检测；3）双层节点架构根据历史行为将二级节点分为可信与不可信，可信节点通过乐观执行实时提供推理结果，不可信节点需经完整共识验证；4）线束层通过心跳探测、确定性重计算异常检测和自动信任管理监控节点行为。

Result: 原型实现实验表明：对篡改记录的检测率为100%，误报率为0%；记录和中心操作的验证延迟为亚毫秒级；线束层能有效收敛，在两轮内排除对抗性节点，在五轮内将诚实节点提升为可信状态。

Conclusion: HadAgent成功实现了将区块链挖矿算力转化为有价值的AI推理服务，通过创新的PoI共识机制、三通道区块结构和双层节点架构，在保证安全性的同时实现了高效的LLM推理服务，为解决PoW资源浪费问题提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Proof-of-Work (PoW) blockchain consensus consumes vast computational resources without producing useful output, while the rapid growth of large language model (LLM) agents has created unprecedented demand for GPU computation. We present HadAgent, a decentralized agentic AI serving system that replaces hash-based mining with Proof-of-Inference (PoI), a consensus mechanism in which nodes earn block-creation rights by executing deterministic LLM inference tasks. Because verification requires only re-executing a single forward pass under identical conditions, cross-node verification operates at consensus speed. HadAgent organizes validated records into a three-lane block body with dedicated DATA, MODEL, and PROOF channels, each protected by an independent Merkle root for fine-grained tamper detection. A two-tier node architecture classifies secondary nodes as trusted or non-trusted based on historical behavior: trusted nodes serve inference results in real time through optimistic execution, while non-trusted nodes must undergo full consensus verification. A harness layer monitors node behavior through heartbeat probes, anomaly detection via deterministic recomputation, and automated trust management, creating a self-correcting feedback loop that isolates malicious or unreliable participants. Experiments on a prototype implementation demonstrate 100% detection rate and 0% false positive rate for tampered records, sub-millisecond validation latency for record and hub operations, and effective harness convergence that excludes adversarial nodes within two rounds while promoting honest nodes to trusted status within five rounds.

</details>


### [8] [Locality, Not Spectral Mixing, Governs Direct Propagation in Distributed Offline Dynamic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18615)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式离线动态规划的通信复杂度，发现局部性是决定通信轮次的根本因素，直接传播算法优于gossip算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解分布式离线动态规划中通信复杂度的根本决定因素，对比直接边界值传播和gossip平均两种范式，探究谱依赖性是否为算法固有属性。

Method: 建立了分布式离线动态规划的通信复杂度分析框架，证明了通用下界，分析了直接边界值传播算法的收敛性，对比了gossip风格拟合值迭代的性能，并扩展到异步系统。

Result: 证明了在直径至少为L_ε的图上，任何方法达到ε精度至少需要L_ε轮；直接传播算法在T轮后达到O(γ^T/(1-γ) + δ/(1-γ))误差；gossip算法额外依赖1/gap(W)；提供了路径拓扑上带宽敏感的下界。

Conclusion: 局部性是分布式离线动态规划的固有通信障碍，而谱依赖性仅是gossip算法的人为产物，直接传播算法能更好地匹配问题内在的复杂度下界。

Abstract: We study the communication complexity of distributed offline dynamic programming, where a fixed batch dataset is partitioned across (M) machines connected by the data-induced dependency graph. We compare two paradigms: direct boundary-value propagation, which follows Bellman dependencies, and gossip averaging, which mixes local estimates. Our results show that **locality** is the fundamental driver of round complexity. In particular, we prove that no method can achieve (\varepsilon)-accuracy in fewer than (L_\varepsilon = \left\lfloor \log(1/2\varepsilon) / \log(1/γ) \right\rfloor) rounds on graphs of diameter at least (L_\varepsilon), and we show that direct propagation matches this scaling up to constants, attaining error (O(γ^T/(1-γ) + δ/(1-γ))) after (T) rounds. In contrast, gossip-style fitted value iteration incurs an additional (1/\mathrm{gap}(W)) dependence in both convergence rate and asymptotic error. We also prove bandwidth-sensitive lower bounds on path topologies and extend the analysis to asynchronous systems with bounded delays. Together, these results show that spectral dependence is an artifact of gossip-based algorithms, whereas locality is the intrinsic barrier in distributed offline dynamic programming.

</details>


### [9] [ARGUS: Agentic GPU Optimization Guided by Data-Flow Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18616)
*Haohui Mai,Xiaoyan Guo,Xiangyun Ding,Daifeng Li,Qiuchu Yu,Chenzhun Guo,Cong Wang,Jiacheng Zhao,Christos Kozyrakis,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Argus框架，通过数据流不变量和编译时验证，使LLM代理生成的GPU内核性能达到手写优化汇编的99-104%，远超现有代理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的编码代理虽能生成功能正确的GPU内核，但在矩阵乘法、注意力机制和混合专家模型等关键计算上，性能远低于手写优化库。现有代理依赖稀疏的通过/失败反馈，无法诊断全局约束违规，而峰值GPU性能需要对平铺、共享内存暂存、软件流水线和指令调度等紧密耦合的优化进行协调推理。

Method: 提出Argus代理框架，核心是数据流不变量——一种编译时规范，用于编码数据在内核执行中的调度方式。引入基于瓦片的Pythonic DSL，暴露硬件指令和编译器策略；提供标签函数传播符号注释，通过标签断言强制约束；违规时编译器返回具体反例（线程、数据元素、程序点），提供密集结构化反馈。通过抽象解释和SMT求解在编译时验证不变量，运行时零开销。结合上下文强化学习规划器选择优化并合成有效不变量。

Result: 在AMD MI300X GPU上评估GEMM、Flash Attention和MoE内核（占LLM推理GPU时间90%以上）。生成的内核达到最先进手写优化汇编吞吐量的99-104%，比现有代理系统快2-1543倍。在200个KernelBench任务上，100%解决Level 1问题，90%解决Level 2问题。

Conclusion: Argus证明了通过编译时数据流不变量验证和密集反馈机制，LLM代理能够生成性能媲美手写优化的GPU内核，解决了现有代理在全局约束诊断方面的关键缺陷，为高性能GPU编程自动化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents can generate functionally correct GPU kernels, yet their performance remains far below hand-optimized libraries on critical computations such as matrix multiplication, attention, and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE). Peak GPU performance requires coordinated reasoning over tightly coupled optimizations, including tiling, shared-memory staging, software pipelining, and instruction scheduling, while existing agents rely on sparse pass/fail feedback, leaving them unable to diagnose global constraint violations.
  We present Argus, an agentic framework that addresses this through data-flow invariants: compile-time specifications encoding how data must be choreographed throughout kernel execution. Argus introduces a tile-based, Pythonic DSL exposing hardware instructions and compiler policies while hiding low-level representations. The DSL provides tag functions to propagate symbolic annotations through data and control flow, and tag assertions to enforce relational constraints at use sites. When violations occur, the compiler returns concrete counterexamples identifying the thread, data element, and program point, enabling dense, structured feedback for targeted fixes. Invariants are verified at compile time via abstract interpretation over a layout algebra and SMT solving, with zero runtime overhead. An in-context reinforcement learning planner learns to select optimizations and synthesize effective invariants, supported by a curated knowledge base of GPU optimization techniques.
  We evaluate Argus on the AMD MI300X GPU across GEMM, flash attention, and MoE kernels accounting for over 90% of GPU time in LLM inference. Generated kernels achieve 99-104% of state-of-the-art hand-optimized assembly throughput and are 2-1543x faster than existing agentic systems. Argus further generalizes to 200 KernelBench tasks, solving 100% of Level 1 and 90% of Level 2 problems.

</details>


### [10] [Unlocking the Edge deployment and ondevice acceleration of multi-LoRA enabled one-for-all foundational LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18655)
*Sravanth Kodavanti,Sowmya Vajrala,Srinivas Miriyala,Utsav Tiwari,Uttam Kumar,Utkarsh Kumar Mahawar,Achal Pratap Singh,Arya D,Narendra Mutyala,Vikram Nelvoy Rajendiran,Sharan Kumar Allur,Euntaik Lee,Dohyoung Kim,HyeonSu Lee,Gyusung Cho,JungBae Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个硬件感知框架，通过集成运行时LoRA、多流解码和动态自推测解码等技术，在三星Galaxy设备上实现了LLM的高效部署，在内存和延迟上获得4-6倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能手机等边缘设备上部署大型语言模型（LLM）面临内存、延迟和运行时灵活性等严峻工程挑战，现有方法难以满足商业化移动平台的需求。

Method: 提出了一个硬件感知框架，包含四个核心技术：1) 将应用特定的LoRA作为运行时输入集成到单一冻结推理图中，实现动态任务切换；2) 多流解码机制，在单次前向传播中并发生成多种风格变体；3) 动态自推测解码(DS2D)，一种无需草稿模型的树状策略预测未来token；4) INT4量化与架构级优化。

Result: 在搭载高通SM8650和SM8750芯片的三星Galaxy S24和S25设备上，多流解码降低延迟高达6倍，DS2D加速解码时间2.3倍，整体系统在内存和延迟上实现4-6倍改进，同时在9种语言和8个任务上保持准确性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在边缘设备上部署多用途LLM的实际可行性，推进了生成式AI在移动平台上的商业化应用前景。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) on smartphones poses significant engineering challenges due to stringent constraints on memory, latency, and runtime flexibility. In this work, we present a hardware-aware framework for efficient on-device inference of a LLaMA-based multilingual foundation model supporting multiple use cases on Samsung Galaxy S24 and S25 devices with SM8650 and SM8750 Qualcomm chipsets respectively. Our approach integrates application-specific LoRAs as runtime inputs to a single frozen inference graph, enabling dynamic task switching without recompilation or memory overhead. We further introduce a multi-stream decoding mechanism that concurrently generates stylistic variations - such as formal, polite, or jovial responses - within a single forward pass, reducing latency by up to 6x. To accelerate token generation, we apply Dynamic Self-Speculative Decoding (DS2D), a tree-based strategy that predicts future tokens without requiring a draft model, yielding up to 2.3x speedup in decode time. Combined with quantization to INT4 and architecture-level optimizations, our system achieves 4-6x overall improvements in memory and latency while maintaining accuracy across 9 languages and 8 tasks. These results demonstrate practical feasibility of deploying multi-use-case LLMs on edge devices, advancing the commercial viability of Generative AI in mobile platforms.

</details>


### [11] [Ocean: Fast Estimation-Based Sparse General Matrix-Matrix Multiplication on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19004)
*Yifan Li,Giulia Guidi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于估计的GPU SpGEMM工作流，用轻量级HyperLogLog估计器替代耗时的符号计算步骤，结合动态策略选择和混合累加器设计，在NVIDIA A100和H100上实现了1.4x-2.8x的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU SpGEMM解决方案普遍采用两遍工作流来处理负载不均衡和减少内存访问，其中符号计算步骤（用于确定每行输出元素数量）平均占用约28%的总运行时间，成为性能瓶颈。本文质疑精确符号计算的必要性，旨在通过估计方法降低该开销。

Method: 提出基于估计的SpGEMM工作流，使用轻量级HyperLogLog估计器替代符号计算步骤；设计动态分析策略选择最优工作流并指导累加器配置；引入混合累加器设计，包括一种同时利用共享内存和全局内存的新型哈希累加器。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100和H100架构上，该方法在各种方阵和长方矩阵上持续优于领先的GPU SpGEMM实现，实现了1.4x-2.8x的加速比。

Conclusion: 通过用估计方法替代精确符号计算，结合动态策略选择和优化的累加器设计，能够显著提升GPU上SpGEMM的性能，证明了估计方法在稀疏矩阵计算中的有效性。

Abstract: In computational science and data analytics, many workloads involve irregular and sparse computations that are inherently difficult to optimize for modern hardware. A key kernel is Sparse General Matrix-Matrix Multiplication (SpGEMM), which underpins simulations, graph analytics, and machine learning applications. SpGEMM exhibits irregular memory access patterns and workload imbalance, making it challenging to achieve high performance on GPUs. Current GPU SpGEMM solutions typically rely on a two-pass workflow to address load imbalance and reduce memory access. The symbolic pass, which determines the number of output elements per row, accounts for roughly 28% of the total runtime on average. In this work, we question the necessity of exact symbolic computation and introduce an estimation-based SpGEMM workflow. Our approach replaces the costly symbolic step with lightweight HyperLogLog estimators, combined with an analysis strategy that dynamically selects the optimal workflow and guides accumulator configuration. In addition, we introduce a hybrid accumulator design, including a novel hash-based accumulator that leverages both shared and global memory. Our approach consistently outperforms leading GPU SpGEMM implementations across a wide range of both square and rectangular matrices, achieving speedups of 1.4x-2.8x on NVIDIA A100 and H100 architectures.

</details>


### [12] [YAIFS: Yet (not) Another Intelligent Fog Simulator: A Framework for Agent-Driven Computing Continuum Modeling & Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19181)
*Isaac Lera,Carlos Guerrero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: YAIFS提出了一个交互式、服务导向的雾计算仿真环境，通过集成模型上下文协议（MCP）作为标准化交互层，实现了异构代理与仿真之间的统一交互，并通过LLM助手和多代理场景验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布式系统仿真工具通常被视为静态工具，交互能力有限，无法支持动态观察、控制和修改仿真执行过程，这限制了AI驱动的实验工作流在云边系统中的应用潜力。

Method: 提出YAIFS（Yet (not) Another Intelligent Fog Simulator），采用分层架构通过统一API和服务接口暴露仿真功能；集成模型上下文协议（MCP）作为代理与仿真之间的标准化交互层，使异构代理能够使用通用工具集访问状态、调用动作和协调行为，解耦代理实验工作流。

Result: 通过两个场景验证了系统能力：(1) 基于LLM的助手实现自然语言控制仿真；(2) 多代理设置中代理监控系统状态并实时调整放置决策。这些场景展示了MCP如何结构化代理-仿真交互并在动态工作负载下实现自适应行为。

Conclusion: 该方法将仿真转变为交互式和可编程环境，为云边系统中的AI驱动实验开辟了新方向，仿真不再是静态评估工具，而是可以与智能代理动态交互的可编程平台。

Abstract: Simulation plays a key role in the design and evaluation of distributed systems, yet it is often treated as a static tool with limited interaction capabilities. In this work, we present Yet (not) Another Intelligent Fog Simulator (YAIFS), and evolution of YAFS that redefines simulation as an interactive, service-oriented environment. YAIFS introduce a layered architecture that exposes the simulation through a unified API and service interface, enabling external entities to observe, control, and modify its execution. A central contribution is the integration of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) as a standardized interaction layer between agents and the simulation. Through MCP, heterogeneous agents can access state, invoke actions and coordinate behavior using a common set of tools, decoupling agent experimentation workflows. We illustrate these capabilities through two scenarios: an LLM-based assistant that enable natural language control of simulations, and a multi-agent setting where agents monitor system conditions and adapt placement decisions at runtime. These scenarios demonstrate how MCP structures agent-simulation interaction and enable adaptive behavior under dynamic workloads. The proposed approach transforms simulation into an interactive and programmable environment, opening new directions for AI-driven experimentation in cloud-edge systems. The implementation is publicly available at: http://github.com/acsicuib/YAIFS

</details>


### [13] [UniEP: Unified Expert-Parallel MoE MegaKernel for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19241)
*Size Zheng,Xuegui Zheng,Li-wen Chang,Jidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了UniEP系统，通过MegaKernels统一MoE通信与计算并保证数值一致性，在Hopper GPU集群上相比SOTA实现1.03-1.38倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数规模呈指数增长，训练资源消耗日益增加。在MoE模型扩展中，专家并行（EP）是关键策略，但现有优化方案存在诸多问题：依赖临时复杂内核、缺乏跨配置适应性、忽视数值稳定性，且难以满足大规模训练的精度要求，在生产框架中应用受限。

Method: 提出UniEP系统，将多种EP优化策略统一为内聚抽象。核心创新包括：1）将MoE通信与计算融合为MegaKernels，将复杂架构调优转化为统一参数搜索空间以实现自动化适应；2）引入确定性token排序机制，即使在激进的重叠调度下也能保证与顺序执行的数值一致性。

Result: 在配备NVIDIA Hopper GPU的集群上进行评估，UniEP相比最先进工作实现1.03×-1.38×的加速，有效缓解通信瓶颈，同时满足生产级LLM训练的严格精度标准。

Conclusion: UniEP成功解决了现有EP优化方案在生产环境中的适应性和稳定性问题，为大语言模型训练提供了一个既高效又可靠的专家并行解决方案，在保证训练精度的前提下显著提升了性能。

Abstract: The exponential growth in Large Language Model (LLM) parameters has transformed model training into an increasingly resource-intensive endeavor. With the stagnation of Moore's Law and the widening disparity between computation throughput and communication bandwidth, expert parallelism (EP) has emerged as a critical strategy for scaling mixture-of-experts (MoE) models. However, despite numerous proposals for optimizing EP, ranging from communication compression to computation-communication overlap, adoption within production-grade frameworks like Megatron-LM remains conservative. Existing solutions often rely on ad-hoc, complex kernels that lack adaptability across diverse optimization configurations and frequently neglect numerical stability, failing to meet the strict precision requirements of large-scale training.
  In this paper, we introduce UniEP, a novel system that unifies diverse EP optimization strategies into a cohesive abstraction. UniEP fuses the MoE communication and computation into MegaKernels, effectively transforming complex architectural tuning into a unified parameter search space for automated adaptability. Crucially, UniEP incorporates a deterministic token ordering mechanism that guarantees numerical consistency with sequential execution, even under aggressive overlap schedules. We evaluate UniEP on GPU clusters equipped with NVIDIA Hopper GPUs. Our results demonstrate that UniEP achieves 1.03$\times$-1.38$\times$ speedups over state-of-the-art work, effectively mitigating communication bottlenecks while maintaining the rigorous accuracy standards required for production LLM training.

</details>


### [14] [A Simple Communication Scheme for Distributed Fast Multipole Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19243)
*Srinath Kailasa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于MPI邻域集合和均匀树的简单层次通信方案，可在最小重设计下将共享内存FMM扩展至分布式内存，在ARCHER2上弱扩展至512节点处理3.2e10个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决将现有高性能共享内存均匀树FMM实现扩展到分布式内存系统时需要大量重设计的问题，目标是保留共享内存优化的同时实现分布式扩展。

Method: 基于MPI邻域集合（neighborhood collectives）和均匀树构建简单的层次通信方案，针对均匀分布点的常见情况进行优化设计。

Result: 在ARCHER2超级计算机上演示了该方法可扩展到极大问题规模，在512节点上实现了3.2e10个均匀分布点的弱扩展性。对于非均匀点分布，简化方案导致渐进扩展性变差，但由于保留了共享内存优化，仍获得实际可用的运行时间。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了共享内存FMM向分布式内存的简单扩展，在均匀点分布场景表现优异，在非均匀场景虽有渐进性能损失但仍具实用价值，验证了简化设计思路的有效性。

Abstract: We present a simple hierarchical communication scheme for distributed Fast Multipole Methods (FMMs) based on MPI neighborhood collectives and uniform trees. The method targets the common case of extending an existing high-performance shared-memory uniform-tree FMM implementation to distributed memory with minimal redesign while preserving any shared memory optimizations optimizations. Benchmarks on the ARCHER2 supercomputer demonstrate that our method can scale to very large problem sizes, we demonstrate weak-scaling up to 3.2e10 uniformly distributed points on 512 nodes of the machine in our largest runs. Our simplifications based on uniform trees result in worse asymptotic scaling for non-uniform points, however we still obtain practically useful runtimes due to the ability to retain our shared memory optimizations.

</details>


### [15] [POLAR-PIC: A Holistic Framework for Matrixized PIC with Co-Designed Compute, Layout, and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19337)
*Yizhuo Rao,Xingjian Cui,Shangzhi Pang,Jiabin Xie,Guangnan Feng,Jinhui Wei,Ziyan Zhang,Languang Gao,Zhenyu Wang,Zhiguang Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了POLAR-PIC框架，通过软硬件协同设计优化PIC模拟，在百万核心规模下实现了高达10.9倍的加速和67.5%的弱扩展效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体物理中的粒子网格（PIC）模拟在扩展性方面存在瓶颈，主要源于粒子-网格交互计算未充分利用新兴矩阵处理单元（MPU）、粒子运动导致的不规则内存访问以及批量同步重分配破坏了长期数据局部性。

Method: 提出了POLAR-PIC协同设计框架，包含三项关键技术：（i）将场插值重构为MPU友好的外积形式；（ii）维护物理有序的粒子布局以保持内存连续性；（iii）将粒子通信与沉积过程重叠以隐藏重分配开销。

Result: 在Exascale超级计算机上评估，相比原生WarpX参考流水线，POLAR-PIC在均匀等离子体中加速达10.9倍，在真实激光离子加速场景中加速达4.4倍。消融研究显示插值和沉积的加速分别为8.0倍和13.2倍，异步通信设计实现了99.1%的重叠率。在超过200万核心的高迁移动态负载下保持67.5%的弱扩展效率。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，面向未来以矩阵为中心的高性能计算系统，整体协同设计对于实现高扩展性至关重要。POLAR-PIC成功解决了传统PIC模拟的关键性能瓶颈，为大规模等离子体模拟提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Particle-in-Cell (PIC) simulations are fundamental to plasma physics but often suffer from limited scalability due to particle-grid interaction bottlenecks and particle redistribution costs. Specifically, the particle-grid interaction computations have not taken full advantage of the emerging Matrix Processing Units (MPUs), the particle motion introduces irregular memory accesses, and the bulk-synchronous redistribution further destroys long-term data locality thereby limiting parallel efficiency. To address these inefficiencies, we present POLAR-PIC, a co-designed framework for large-scale PIC simulations that (i) reformulates Field Interpolation into an MPU-friendly outer-product form, (ii) maintains a physically ordered particle layout to preserve memory contiguity, and (iii) overlaps particle communication with Deposition to hide redistribution overhead. The evaluation on the pilot system of an Exascale supercomputer demonstrates that POLAR-PIC accelerates the entire particle-processing phase by up to 10.9x in uniform plasma and 4.4x in real-world laser-ion acceleration scenarios compared to the native WarpX reference pipeline on LX2. Ablation studies reveal that the speedups achieved by Interpolation and Deposition are 8.0x and 13.2x, respectively, and the asynchronous communication design sustains a 99.1% overlap ratio. In cross-platform comparisons, POLAR-PIC achieves 13.2% of theoretical peak efficiency on the CPU-based LS system, while WarpX reaches 9.6% on NVIDIA A800 GPUs. Notably, the scalability evaluation demonstrates that POLAR-PIC maintains 67.5% weak scaling efficiency on over 2 million cores under high-migration dynamic workloads, highlighting the importance of holistic co-design for future matrix-centric HPC systems.

</details>


### [16] [CROWDio: A Practical Mobile Crowd Computing Framework with Developer-Oriented Design, Adaptive Scheduling, and Fault Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19363)
*Lakshani Manamperi,Disumi Pathirana,Thiwanka Pathirana,Nipun Premarathna,Kutila Gunasekara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CROWDio平台，通过声明式SDK、分层检查点和多准则调度解决了移动群计算中的开发复杂性、容错性和设备异构性问题，实验表明该平台能显著降低执行时间并保证公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动群计算（MCdC）虽能利用智能手机的闲置算力实现大规模分布式任务处理，但由于缺乏面向开发者的框架来透明地管理设备异构性、容错性和连接不稳定性，其在现实世界中的广泛采用受到限制。

Method: 提出了一个名为CROWDio的集中式MCdC平台，包含三个紧密集成的子系统：（i）声明式SDK，通过单函数注解抽象分布式执行，无需显式并行管理；（ii）分层检查点机制，在移动运行时的内存和执行约束下实现容错任务恢复；（iii）基于实时设备遥测的可插拔多准则调度框架，支持在不修改调度核心的情况下互换决策策略。

Result: 在六台异构Android设备上的实证评估显示，相比朴素的轮询调度，感知能力的自适应调度将总执行时间减少了56.9%；检查点子系统无论检查频率如何，每个任务仅产生2-3秒的有限开销；系统整体的Jain公平性指数达到0.889，证明了在异构工作设备间实现了公平且稳定的工作负载分配。

Conclusion: CROWDio平台通过提供抽象化的开发接口、高效的容错机制和智能的调度策略，成功降低了移动群计算的开发门槛，提升了系统性能与稳定性，为移动群计算的实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Mobile Crowd Computing (MCdC) leverages the idle computational capacity of consumer smartphones to enable distributed task processing at scale; however, widespread real-world adoption remains constrained by the absence of developer-oriented frameworks capable of transparently managing device heterogeneity, fault tolerance, and connectivity volatility. This paper introduces CROWDio, a centralized MCdC platform comprising three tightly integrated subsystems: (i) a declarative SDK that abstracts distributed execution to a single function annotation, eliminating the need for explicit parallelism management; (ii) a tiered checkpointing mechanism that enables fault-tolerant task resumption under the memory and execution constraints inherent to mobile runtimes; and (iii) a pluggable multi-criteria scheduling framework driven by continuous live device telemetry, supporting interchangeable decision strategies without modification to the dispatch core. Empirical evaluation across six heterogeneous Android devices spanning CPU-bound, AI/NLP inference, and data-parallel workloads demonstrates that capability-aware adaptive scheduling reduces total execution time by up to 56.9% relative to naive round-robin dispatch, while the checkpointing subsystem incurs a bounded overhead of only 2-3 s per task regardless of checkpoint frequency. A system-wide Jain's Fairness Index of 0.889 confirms equitable and stable workload distribution across heterogeneous worker devices.

</details>

*Ken Kennedy,Iman Evazzade*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究使用分层方式运行的自主稳定算法来确定宏观层面的知识产权风险，旨在支持所有已定义的知识产权维度并寻求次优解以最小化风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决宏观层面知识产权风险评估问题，需要找到能够支持所有已定义知识产权维度的解决方案，同时通过寻求次优解来最小化风险。

Method: 采用分层方式运行的自主稳定算法（self-stabilizing algorithms）来确定知识产权风险，该方法能够在宏观层面进行风险分析。

Conclusion: 分层自主稳定算法为宏观层面的知识产权风险评估提供了一种有效方法，能够在保证解决方案全面性的同时实现风险最小化。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine the use of self-stabilizing algorithms, operating in a hierarchical manner, to determine intellectual property risks at a macro level. We are both interested in finding a solution that will support all defined intellectual property dimensions as well as suboptimal solutions in order to minimize risk.

</details>


### [18] [DPC: A Distributed Page Cache over CXL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19494)
*Shai Bergman,Zhe Yang,Julien Eudine,Giorgio Negro,Onur Mutlu,Arash Tavakkol,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DPC，一种基于CXL 3.0的操作系统级分布式页缓存。它将集群内存视为单一缓存资源，通过单副本约束和远程映射消除了数据冗余和一致性开销，相比传统方法实现了最高12.4倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式文件系统采用非协调的节点本地页缓存，虽然保证了快速本地访问，但存在两大缺陷：大量数据冗余导致集群DRAM容量利用率低；基于锁的重量级协议产生极高的一致性开销。

Method: 设计并实现了分布式页缓存（DPC）。核心策略是强制执行页粒度的单副本不变式：每个文件页在集群中仅有一个拥有者节点持有DRAM副本，其他节点通过CXL 3.0内存语义进行远程映射访问，而非创建本地副本。系统在CXL仿真框架上进行了端到端实现。

Result: 在真实世界和代表性的数据共享工作负载测试中，DPC实现了最高12.4倍的加速，几何平均加速比为5.6倍。

Conclusion: 利用CXL 3.0内存语义构建的操作系统级分布式页缓存能够有效解决传统架构的资源浪费和性能瓶颈问题，在保持标准文件系统接口和语义的前提下显著提升了性能。

Abstract: Modern distributed file systems rely on uncoordinated, per node page caches that replicate hot data locally across the cluster. While ensuring fast local access, this architecture underutilizes aggregate cluster DRAM capacity through massive data redundancy and incurs prohibitive coherence overhead via heavyweight, lock-based protocols. In this paper, we focus on the design of a distributed page cache that treats the entire cluster's main memory as a single cache budget while preserving standard file-system interfaces and semantics. We present Distributed Page Cache (DPC), an OS-level, distributed page cache built on top of Compute Express Link (CXL) 3.0 memory semantics. DPC enforces a single-copy invariant at page granularity: each file page has exactly one owner node holding the sole resident DRAM copy, and other nodes access it via CXL-based remote mappings rather than creating replicas of the page. DPC is implemented end-to-end on a CXL-based emulation framework that models multi-host CXL 3.0 memory fabrics, enabling detailed evaluation in the absence of widespread hardware. Across real-world and representative data-sharing workloads, DPC delivers speedups of up to 12.4X, with a geometric-mean speedup of 5.6X.

</details>


### [19] [ReaLB: Real-Time Load Balancing for Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19503)
*Yingping Wang,Yi Wu,Xiangyu Wu,Junwei Cui,Weilin Cai,Zhijiang Guo,Jiayi Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ReaLB，一种面向多模态MoE推理的实时负载均衡方法，通过动态调整计算精度实现1.29倍加速，精度损失控制在1.2%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态MoE模型在推理过程中，不同模态的专家负载高度动态且分布不均。在预填充阶段，视觉Token通常占据主导地位，导致专家并行（EP）下出现严重的负载不平衡，部分设备过载，从而降低整体系统吞吐量。

Method: 提出ReaLB方法，在运行时以每个EP秩为单位动态调整MoE专家的计算精度。针对被视觉密集型专家主导的秩，分配较低精度计算以利用FP4 Tensor Cores提升执行效率。该方法无需冗余专家或额外内存分配，而是即时执行逐层专家精度转换，并将相关开销隐藏在MoE计算前的分发阶段。

Result: 在代表性多模态MoE模型上的实验表明，ReaLB实现了1.29倍的层级加速，同时将精度损失限制在1.2%以内。

Conclusion: ReaLB成功解决了多模态MoE推理中的负载不平衡问题，在几乎不损失精度的情况下显著提升了推理效率，为零开销的实时负载均衡提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are widely used in modern large language models and multimodal models. However, inference efficiency is often limited by highly dynamic and skewed expert workloads across different modalities. During the prefill stage with large batch sizes, vision tokens frequently dominate the input sequences. Under expert parallelism (EP), this leads to severe load imbalance, where a subset of devices becomes overloaded, reducing overall system throughput.
  We propose ReaLB, a real-time load balancing method for multimodal MoE (MMoE) inference that introduces zero scheduling overhead. ReaLB dynamically adjusts the computation precision of MoE experts at runtime on a per-EP-rank basis. For ranks dominated by vision-heavy experts, ReaLB assigns lower-precision computation to improve execution efficiency by exploiting FP4 Tensor Cores. ReaLB does not require redundant experts or additional memory allocation. Instead, it performs layer-wise expert precision transformation on the fly and hides the associated overhead within the dispatch phase before MoE computation. Experiments on representative MMoE models show that ReaLB achieves 1.29x layer-level speedup while limiting accuracy loss to within 1.2%.

</details>


### [20] [FEPLB: Exploiting Copy Engines for Nearly Free MoE Load Balancing in Distributed Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19654)
*Shuyao Qi,Haoyuan Liu,Shizhen Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出FEPLB，利用NVIDIA Hopper架构的NVLink Copy Engine实现近乎零开销的细粒度MoE负载均衡，显著减少token与GEMM拖尾问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数MoE细粒度动态调度方案需引入额外通信开销且难以隐藏，特别是在DeepEP等现代批量传输后端上，实现高效负载均衡与低通信成本面临矛盾。

Method: 利用NVIDIA Hopper架构NVLink Copy Engine可在不占用SM周期的情况下实现GPU节点内数据传输，构建与计算并行的通信通道。FEPLB采用两阶段分发：第一阶段通过标准EP后端进行节点间路由；第二阶段通过Copy Engine在NVLink域内以近零成本重分配动态专家的token与权重，轻量级CPU调度器与静态专家计算并发执行。

Result: 在GLM-5的MoE层（128专家、无辅助损失、最多16块H100 GPU）上，token拖尾降低51-70%，GEMM拖尾降低50-68%，且无显著EP通信开销。在EP=8时，token拖尾比FasterMoE低2倍，优势随EP并行度提升而增强。

Conclusion: FEPLB成功将空闲硬件转化为MoE负载重均衡的新并行维度，与现有EP及PP并行策略无冲突、无需重配置即可共存，实现了高效的细粒度负载均衡。

Abstract: Fine-grained, per-micro-batch load balancing is essential for efficient Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training, yet every prior dynamic scheduling scheme pays for it with extra communication that is hard to hide. Especially on modern bulk-transfer backends such as DeepEP. We make a simple but consequential observation: on the NVIDIA Hopper architecture the NVLink Copy Engine can move data between intra-node GPUs without consuming any SM cycles, effectively providing a nearly free communication channel that runs in parallel with compute kernels. FEPLB turns this idle hardware into a new parallel dimension for MoE load rebalancing. Its Two-Phase Dispatch first routes tokens across nodes via the standard EP backend, then redistributes dynamic-expert tokens and weights within the NVLink domain through the Copy Engine at nearly zero cost, while a lightweight CPU scheduler runs concurrently with static expert computation. Because FEPLB uses only Copy Engine and CPU that are orthogonal to those consumed by EP and PP, it coexists with existing parallel strategies without reconfiguration.
  On GLM-5's MoE layers (128 experts, no auxiliary loss, up to 16 H100 GPUs), FEPLB reduces the token straggler by 51-70% and the GEMM straggler by 50-68% with no measurable EP communication overhead. Its advantage grows with the EP degree: at EP=8, it achieves 2x lower token straggler than FasterMoE.

</details>

### [21] [On Solving the Multiple Variable Gapped Longest Common Subsequence Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18645)
*Marko Djukanović,Nikola Balaban,Christian Blum,Aleksandar Kartelj,Sašo Džeroski,Žiga Zebec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次针对变间隙最长公共子序列（VGLCS）问题进行了全面的计算研究，提出了一种基于根状态图的迭代束搜索框架，有效解决了状态空间组合爆炸问题，在320个合成实例上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGLCS问题是经典最长公共子序列（LCS）问题的推广，允许在子序列的连续字符之间引入灵活的间隙约束。该问题在分子序列比较（残基间的结构距离约束）和时间序列分析（事件间的特定时间延迟）等实际应用中具有重要意义。

Method: 提出了一种基于根状态图表示的搜索框架，状态空间由大量根状态子图组成。为应对组合爆炸问题，采用了迭代束搜索策略，动态维护一个全局的有前途候选根节点池，以有效控制迭代的多样性。此外，将LCS文献中的多种已知启发式算法集成到独立的束搜索过程中以提升解的质量。

Result: 进行了首个针对VGLCS问题的全面计算研究，包含多达10条输入序列、每条序列长达500个字符的320个合成实例。实验结果表明，所设计的方法在相近的运行时间内，相比基准束搜索算法具有更强的鲁棒性和性能优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于根状态图的迭代束搜索框架能够有效解决VGLCS问题中的组合爆炸挑战，在多样化启发式的辅助下实现了高质量的解，为该领域提供了首个基准计算研究结果，证明了方法的有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the Variable Gapped Longest Common Subsequence (VGLCS) problem, a generalization of the classical LCS problem involving flexible gap constraints between consecutive solutions' characters. The problem arises in molecular sequence comparison, where structural distance constraints between residues must be respected, and in time-series analysis where events are required to occur within specified temporal delays. We propose a search framework based on the root-based state graph representation, in which the state space comprises a generally large number of rooted state subgraphs. To cope with the resulting combinatorial explosion, an iterative beam search strategy is employed, dynamically maintaining a global pool of promising candidate root nodes, enabling effective control of diversification across iterations. To exploit the search for high-quality solutions, several known heuristics from the LCS literature are utilized into the standalone beam search procedure. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first comprehensive computational study on the VGLCS problem comprising 320 synthetic instances with up to 10 input sequences and up to 500 characters. Experimental results show robustness of the designed approach over the baseline beam search in comparable runtimes.

</details>


### [22] [Beyond One Output: Visualizing and Comparing Distributions of Language Model Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18724)
*Emily Reif,Claire Yang,Jared Hwang,Deniz Nazar,Noah Smith,Jeff Heer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GROVE，一个将多个语言模型生成结果可视化为文本图中重叠路径的交互式工具，解决了用户仅通过单一输出难以理解模型分布特性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户通常通过单一输出与语言模型交互并据此评估模型，但这种交互方式隐藏了分布结构（如模式、罕见边缘情况、对提示词微小变化的敏感性），导致用户在开放式任务迭代中容易以偏概全。作者旨在揭示这些隐藏的分布特性。

Method: 基于对13名研究人员使用语言模型习惯的形成性研究，开发了GROVE交互式可视化系统。该系统将多个LM生成结果表示为文本图中的重叠路径，展示共享结构、分支点和聚类，同时保留对原始输出的访问。通过三项众包用户研究（参与者分别为47、44、40人）评估其在不同分布任务中的表现。

Result: 实验结果支持一种混合工作流：图形摘要（graph summaries）能显著改善结构化判断任务，例如评估多样性；而直接检查原始输出在处理细节导向的问题上表现更优。

Conclusion: GROVE成功地将语言模型的分布特性可视化，为用户提供了一种有效的方法来理解生成结果的多样性及结构，证明了结合图形概览与原始输出检查的混合方法在不同任务中的互补优势。

Abstract: Users typically interact with and evaluate language models via single outputs, but each output is just one sample from a broad distribution of possible completions. This interaction hides distributional structure such as modes, uncommon edge cases, and sensitivity to small prompt changes, leading users to over-generalize from anecdotes when iterating on prompts for open-ended tasks. Informed by a formative study with researchers who use LMs (n=13) examining when stochasticity matters in practice, how they reason about distributions over language, and where current workflows break down, we introduce GROVE. GROVE is an interactive visualization that represents multiple LM generations as overlapping paths through a text graph, revealing shared structure, branching points, and clusters while preserving access to raw outputs. We evaluate across three crowdsourced user studies (N=47, 44, and 40 participants) targeting complementary distributional tasks. Our results support a hybrid workflow: graph summaries improve structural judgments such as assessing diversity, while direct output inspection remains stronger for detail-oriented questions.

</details>


### [23] [ARES: Adaptive Red-Teaming and End-to-End Repair of Policy-Reward System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18789)
*Jiacheng Liang,Yao Ma,Tharindu Kumarage,Satyapriya Krishna,Rahul Gupta,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan,Charith Peris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARES框架，通过同时发现并修复核心大语言模型和奖励模型中的系统性弱点，增强RLHF的安全对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLHF安全机制存在关键漏洞：当奖励模型（RM）未能惩罚不安全行为时，会成为单点故障。现有红队测试主要针对策略层面的弱点，却忽视了系统性弱点——即核心LLM和RM同时失效的情况。

Method: ARES框架采用“安全导师”机制，动态组合主题、角色、策略和目标等结构化组件类型，生成语义连贯的对抗性提示词及对应的恶意与安全回复，以此同时暴露核心LLM和RM的弱点；随后实施两阶段修复：首先微调RM以更好地检测有害内容，再利用改进后的RM优化核心模型。

Result: 在多个对抗性安全基准测试上的实验表明，ARES在保持模型能力的同时，显著提升了安全鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ARES建立了一个全面的RLHF安全对齐新范式，通过系统性地发现并缓解双重漏洞，有效解决了现有方法忽视系统性弱点的问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is central to aligning Large Language Models (LLMs), yet it introduces a critical vulnerability: an imperfect Reward Model (RM) can become a single point of failure when it fails to penalize unsafe behaviors. While existing red-teaming approaches primarily target policy-level weaknesses, they overlook what we term systemic weaknesses cases where both the core LLM and the RM fail in tandem.
  We present ARES, a framework that systematically discovers and mitigates such dual vulnerabilities. ARES employs a ``Safety Mentor'' that dynamically composes semantically coherent adversarial prompts by combining structured component types (topics, personas, tactics, goals) and generates corresponding malicious and safe responses. This dual-targeting approach exposes weaknesses in both the core LLM and the RM simultaneously. Using the vulnerabilities gained, ARES implements a two-stage repair process: first fine-tuning the RM to better detect harmful content, then leveraging the improved RM to optimize the core model. Experiments across multiple adversarial safety benchmarks demonstrate that ARES substantially enhances safety robustness while preserving model capabilities, establishing a new paradigm for comprehensive RLHF safety alignment.

</details>


### [24] [AI scientists produce results without reasoning scientifically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18805)
*Martiño Ríos-García,Nawaf Alampara,Chandan Gupta,Indrajeet Mandal,Sajid Mannan,Ali Asghar Aghajani,N. M. Anoop Krishnan,Kevin Maik Jablonka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前LLM科学智能体虽然能执行科研流程，但严重缺乏科学推理所需的认识论规范（如证据忽视率高达68%），这种缺陷源于基础模型本身，无法通过外部框架工程修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的科学智能体被广泛用于自主科研，其推理过程是否遵循科学探究的自我修正性认识论规范尚不清楚。本文旨在评估这些智能体的推理结构是否符合科学认识论标准。

Method: 在8个领域（涵盖工作流执行和假设驱动探究）中进行超过25,000次智能体运行测试。采用双重视角分析：(i)系统性性能分析，解构基础模型与智能体框架的贡献；(ii)智能体推理的认识论结构行为分析。

Result: 基础模型是性能和行为的主要决定因素（解释方差占41.4%，框架仅占1.5%）。所有配置中，68%的轨迹忽略证据，仅26%发生反驳驱动的信念修正，趋同多重测试证据罕见。即使在提供近乎完整的成功推理轨迹作为上下文时，这些推理模式仍然存在，且在认识论要求高的领域中不可靠性会累积。

Conclusion: 当前基于LLM的智能体能执行科学工作流，但不具备表征科学推理的认识论模式。基于结果的评估无法检测这些失败，单独的框架工程无法修复。在推理本身成为训练目标之前，此类智能体产生的科学知识无法通过其生成过程来证明其合理性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based systems are increasingly deployed to conduct scientific research autonomously, yet whether their reasoning adheres to the epistemic norms that make scientific inquiry self-correcting is poorly understood. Here, we evaluate LLM-based scientific agents across eight domains, spanning workflow execution to hypothesis-driven inquiry, through more than 25,000 agent runs and two complementary lenses: (i) a systematic performance analysis that decomposes the contributions of the base model and the agent scaffold, and (ii) a behavioral analysis of the epistemological structure of agent reasoning. We observe that the base model is the primary determinant of both performance and behavior, accounting for 41.4% of explained variance versus 1.5% for the scaffold. Across all configurations, evidence is ignored in 68% of traces, refutation-driven belief revision occurs in 26%, and convergent multi-test evidence is rare. The same reasoning pattern appears whether the agent executes a computational workflow or conducts hypothesis-driven inquiry. They persist even when agents receive near-complete successful reasoning trajectories as context, and the resulting unreliability compounds across repeated trials in epistemically demanding domains. Thus, current LLM-based agents execute scientific workflows but do not exhibit the epistemic patterns that characterize scientific reasoning. Outcome-based evaluation cannot detect these failures, and scaffold engineering alone cannot repair them. Until reasoning itself becomes a training target, the scientific knowledge produced by such agents cannot be justified by the process that generated it.

</details>


### [25] [Quantum inspired qubit qutrit neural networks for real time financial forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18838)
*Kanishk Bakshi,Kathiravan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对比了人工神经网络(ANN)、量子量子位神经网络(QQBN)和量子量子三能级神经网络(QQTN)在股票预测中的表现，发现QQTN在准确率、风险调整收益、预测一致性和训练效率等方面均优于经典模型和量子位模型，准确率超过70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究机器学习模型在股票预测中的性能表现，特别是比较经典人工神经网络与量子启发神经网络（量子位和量子三能级）在预测准确性、训练效率和稳健性方面的差异，以验证量子方法在金融实时计算场景中的应用潜力。

Method: 研究系统梳理了三类模型的架构设计、方法论和训练流程，通过实验对比ANN、QQBN和QQTN在训练时间、预测准确率、夏普比率（风险调整收益）和信息系数（预测质量一致性）等指标上的表现，并评估了在不同市场条件下的稳健性。

Result: 所有模型准确率均超过70%。量子三能级神经网络(QQTN)在多个维度表现最优：夏普比率更高，信息系数显示预测质量更稳定，在多变市场环境下稳健性更强。QQTN同时实现了与经典方法相当甚至更优的性能，且训练时间显著缩短。QQTN全面超越了经典ANN和量子位QQBN模型。

Conclusion: 量子三能级神经网络在金融股票预测中展现出显著优势，具备更优的准确性、效率和适应性。该研究证明了量子启发方法在实际金融应用中的变革性潜力，特别适用于对实时性要求高的计算密集型场景，为量子神经网络在金融领域的集成应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This research investigates the performance and efficacy of machine learning models in stock prediction, comparing Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs), Quantum Qubit-based Neural Networks (QQBNs), and Quantum Qutrit-based Neural Networks (QQTNs). By outlining methodologies, architectures, and training procedures, the study highlights significant differences in training times and performance metrics across models. While all models demonstrate robust accuracies above 70%, the Quantum Qutrit-based Neural Network consistently outperforms with advantages in risk-adjusted returns, measured by the Sharpe ratio, greater consistency in prediction quality through the Information Coefficient, and enhanced robustness under varying market conditions. The QQTN not only surpasses its classical and qubit-based counterparts in multiple quantitative and qualitative metrics but also achieves comparable performance with significantly reduced training times. These results showcase the promising prospects of Quantum Qutrit-based Neural Networks in practical financial applications, where real-time processing is critical. By achieving superior accuracy, efficiency, and adaptability, the proposed models underscore the transformative potential of quantum-inspired approaches, paving the way for their integration into computationally intensive fields.

</details>


### [26] [Human-Guided Harm Recovery for Computer Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18847)
*Christy Li,Sky CH-Wang,Andi Peng,Andreea Bobu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对语言模型智能体在计算机系统上执行有害操作后的补救问题，提出了“伤害恢复”框架，通过用户研究构建偏好对齐的恢复标准，训练奖励模型对恢复计划进行重排序，并推出包含50个任务的BackBench基准进行系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型智能体获得在真实计算机系统上执行操作的能力，现有研究主要聚焦于规模化的有害行为预防，但在预防失效后如何有效补救伤害的问题被忽视。缺乏从有害状态安全恢复到正常状态的方法，成为智能体安全领域的重要缺口。

Method: 1) 通过形成性用户研究识别用户重视的恢复维度，构建自然语言评估标准；2) 收集1,150组成对偏好判断数据集，揭示情境依赖的属性重要性变化；3) 基于学习到的洞察训练奖励模型，在测试时对智能体生成的多个候选恢复计划进行重排序；4) 引入BackBench基准（50个计算机使用任务）系统评估恢复能力。

Result: 人类评估表明，基于奖励模型的脚手架方法生成的恢复轨迹质量优于基础智能体和基于规则的脚手架方法。数据集分析揭示了用户偏好的情境依赖性，例如在特定场景下更倾向于务实、针对性的策略而非全面长期的方案。

Conclusion: 本研究奠定了新型智能体安全方法的基础——这类方法不仅通过预防来应对伤害，还能以对齐和意图为导向来处理伤害后果。这为构建更安全、更可靠的智能体系统提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: As LM agents gain the ability to execute actions on real computer systems, we need ways to not only prevent harmful actions at scale but also effectively remediate harm when prevention fails. We formalize a solution to this neglected challenge in post-execution safeguards as harm recovery: the problem of optimally steering an agent from a harmful state back to a safe one in alignment with human preferences. We ground preference-aligned recovery through a formative user study that identifies valued recovery dimensions and produces a natural language rubric. Our dataset of 1,150 pairwise judgments reveals context-dependent shifts in attribute importance, such as preferences for pragmatic, targeted strategies over comprehensive long-term approaches. We operationalize these learned insights in a reward model, re-ranking multiple candidate recovery plans generated by an agent scaffold at test time. To evaluate recovery capabilities systematically, we introduce BackBench, a benchmark of 50 computer-use tasks that test an agent's ability to recover from harmful states. Human evaluation shows our reward model scaffold yields higher-quality recovery trajectories than base agents and rubric-based scaffolds. Together, these contributions lay the foundation for a new class of agent safety methods -- ones that confront harm not only by preventing it, but by navigating its aftermath with alignment and intent.

</details>


### [27] [From Natural Language to Executable Narsese: A Neuro-Symbolic Benchmark and Pipeline for Reasoning with NARS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18873)
*Mina Gabriel,Pei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个神经符号框架，通过将自然语言推理问题转化为可执行的一阶逻辑(FOL)和Narsese形式表示，并发布了NARS-Reasoning-v0.1基准数据集，以支持更可靠的神经符号推理系统开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLMs)在语言生成方面表现出色，但在需要显式符号结构、多步推理和可解释不确定性的推理任务中仍然不可靠。作者旨在通过神经符号框架解决这些局限性，使LLMs能够生成可执行的符号表示而非仅产生最终的语言响应。

Method: 1) 创建NARS-Reasoning-v0.1基准数据集，包含自然语言推理问题及其FOL形式、可执行Narsese程序和三分类标签(True/False/Uncertain)；2) 开发从FOL到可执行Narsese的确定性编译流程；3) 通过ONA运行时执行验证符号目标的正确性；4) 提出语言结构感知(LSP)方法，训练LLM生成推理相关的符号结构；5) 训练Phi-2 LoRA适配器进行三分类推理任务。

Result: 成功构建了经过运行时执行验证的基准数据集，证明符号目标不仅语法正确且与预期答案行为一致；Phi-2 LoRA适配器的训练结果表明该基准能够支持监督适应和可执行评估。

Conclusion: 可执行符号生成和基于执行的验证是构建更可靠神经符号推理系统的实用路径，为未来研究提供了新的方向和工具支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are highly capable at language generation, but they remain unreliable when reasoning requires explicit symbolic structure, multi-step inference, and interpretable uncertainty. This paper presents a neuro-symbolic framework for translating natural-language reasoning problems into executable formal representations using first-order logic (FOL) and Narsese, the language of the Non-Axiomatic Reasoning System (NARS). To support this direction, we introduce NARS-Reasoning-v0.1, a benchmark of natural-language reasoning problems paired with FOL forms, executable Narsese programs, and three gold labels: True, False, and Uncertain. We develop a deterministic compilation pipeline from FOL to executable Narsese and validate retained examples through runtime execution in OpenNARS for Applications (ONA), ensuring that the symbolic targets are not only syntactically well formed but also behaviorally aligned with the intended answer. We further present Language-Structured Perception (LSP), a formulation in which an LLM is trained to produce reasoning-relevant symbolic structure rather than only a final verbal response. As an initial proof of concept, we also train and release a Phi-2 LoRA adapter on NARS-Reasoning-v0.1 for three-label reasoning classification, showing that the benchmark can support supervised adaptation in addition to executable evaluation. Overall, the paper positions executable symbolic generation and execution-based validation as a practical path toward more reliable neuro-symbolic reasoning systems.

</details>


### [28] [Explicit Trait Inference for Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19278)
*Suhaib Abdurahman,Etsuko Ishii,Katerina Margatina,Divya Bhargavi,Monica Sunkara,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了显式特质推理（ETI）方法，通过让LLM智能体从交互历史中推断并追踪合作伙伴的心理特征（温暖度和能力），显著改善了多智能体系统的协调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在处理复杂任务时容易出现协调失败问题，如目标漂移、错误级联和行为不一致，缺乏对合作伙伴特征的理解和追踪机制。

Method: 提出显式特质推理（ETI）方法，该方法基于心理学理论，使智能体能够沿着温暖度（如信任）和能力（如技能）两个心理维度，从交互历史中推断和追踪合作伙伴的特征，并以此指导决策。

Result: 在控制环境（经济博弈）中减少45-77%的收益损失，在复杂多智能体环境（MultiAgentBench）中性能提升3-29%；ETI特征画像能有效预测智能体行为，信息丰富的特征画像驱动了性能改进。

Conclusion: ETI作为一种轻量级且稳健的机制，能够有效改善多样化多智能体环境中的协调能力；研究首次系统性证明了LLM智能体能够从交互历史中可靠推断他人特征，并利用结构化的特征认知来实现更好的协调。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) show promise on complex tasks but remain prone to coordination failures such as goal drift, error cascades, and misaligned behaviors. We propose Explicit Trait Inference (ETI), a psychologically grounded method for improving coordination. ETI enables agents to infer and track partner characteristics along two established psychological dimensions--warmth (e.g., trust) and competence (e.g., skill)--from interaction histories to guide decisions. We evaluate ETI in controlled settings (economic games), where it reduces payoff loss by 45-77%, and in more realistic, complex multi-agent settings (MultiAgentBench), where it improves performance by 3-29% depending on the scenario and model, relative to a CoT baseline. Additional analysis shows that gains are closely linked to trait inference: ETI profiles predict agents' actions, and informative profiles drive improvements. These results highlight ETI as a lightweight and robust mechanism for improving coordination in diverse multi-agent settings, and provide the first systematic evidence that LLM agents can (i) reliably infer others' traits from interaction histories and (ii) leverage structured awareness of others' traits for coordination.

</details>


### [29] [How Adversarial Environments Mislead Agentic AI?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18874)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Huichi Zhou,Zhenhao Li,Peiyuan Jing,Krinos Li,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了工具集成智能体存在“信任鸿沟”，即缺乏对恶意工具输出的怀疑能力。作者提出了对抗性环境注入（AEI）威胁模型，通过POTEMKIN框架测试发现，智能体在面对广度攻击（幻觉）和深度攻击（迷宫）时存在严重的鲁棒性缺陷，且对一种攻击的抵抗力往往增加对另一种攻击的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对工具集成智能体的评估仅关注其在良性环境中的能力表现，从未考虑“工具撒谎”的情况。智能体被部署的前提是外部工具能将其输出锚定在现实中，但这种依赖性本身构成了一个关键的攻击面。作者旨在填补这一评估空白，研究智能体在面对被恶意篡改的工具输出时的表现。

Method: 1. 形式化定义了“对抗性环境注入”（AEI）威胁模型，模拟攻击者通过篡改工具输出来欺骗智能体；
2. 开发了POTEMKIN框架（兼容模型上下文协议MCP），用于即插即用的鲁棒性测试；
3. 设计了两种正交的攻击面：
   - “幻觉”（广度攻击）：通过污染检索结果诱导认知漂移，使其产生错误信念；
   - “迷宫”（深度攻击）：利用结构性陷阱导致策略崩溃，陷入无限循环。

Result: 在五个前沿智能体上进行了超过11,000次测试，发现了一个显著的鲁棒性鸿沟：智能体对一种攻击的抵抗能力往往会增加其对另一种攻击的脆弱性。这证明了认知鲁棒性（对抗错误信念）和导航鲁棒性（对抗结构性陷阱）是两种截然不同的能力，智能体无法同时兼顾。

Conclusion: 当前的智能体评估体系存在根本性缺陷，仅关注性能而忽视了对工具输出的怀疑能力。认知鲁棒性和导航鲁棒性是两个独立的维度，未来在构建可靠的工具集成智能体时，必须同时关注这两个方面，而非仅仅优化单一性能指标。

Abstract: Tool-integrated agents are deployed on the premise that external tools ground their outputs in reality. Yet this very reliance creates a critical attack surface. Current evaluations benchmark capability in benign settings, asking "can the agent use tools correctly" but never "what if the tools lie". We identify this Trust Gap: agents are evaluated for performance, not for skepticism. We formalize this vulnerability as Adversarial Environmental Injection (AEI), a threat model where adversaries compromise tool outputs to deceive agents. AEI constitutes environmental deception: constructing a "fake world" of poisoned search results and fabricated reference networks around unsuspecting agents. We operationalize this via POTEMKIN, a Model Context Protocol (MCP)-compatible harness for plug-and-play robustness testing. We identify two orthogonal attack surfaces: The Illusion (breadth attacks) poison retrieval to induce epistemic drift toward false beliefs, while The Maze (depth attacks) exploit structural traps to cause policy collapse into infinite loops. Across 11,000+ runs on five frontier agents, we find a stark robustness gap: resistance to one attack often increases vulnerability to the other, demonstrating that epistemic and navigational robustness are distinct capabilities.

</details>


### [30] [Large Language Models Exhibit Normative Conformity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19301)
*Mikako Bito,Keita Nishimoto,Kimitaka Asatani,Ichiro Sakata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究区分了大语言模型中的信息性从众与规范性从众，发现多数LLM存在这两种从众倾向，且规范性从众可被操控，揭示了LLM-MAS决策的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型表现出的从众偏差对基于LLM的多智能体系统决策构成重大挑战。现有研究仅将从众视为简单的观点改变，缺乏对从众机制的深入理解。因此，本研究引入社会心理学中信息性从众与规范性从众的区分，旨在从机制层面理解LLM的从众行为。

Method: 设计新任务区分两种从众：信息性从众（参与者旨在做出准确判断）和规范性从众（参与者旨在避免冲突或获得群体接纳）。基于这些任务设置进行实验，评估六种LLM，并操纵社会情境的细微方面以测试对规范性从众目标的影响。此外，分析与信息性和规范性从众相关的内部向量。

Result: 在评估的六种LLM中，多达五种不仅表现出信息性从众，还表现出规范性从众倾向。通过操纵社会情境的细微方面，可以控制特定LLM将规范性从众指向的目标。分析表明，尽管两种行为在外部表现为相同的“从众”形式，但可能由不同的内部机制驱动。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明LLM-MAS中的决策可能容易受到少数恶意用户的操纵。本研究为理解“规范”如何在LLM中实现以及它们如何影响群体动态提供了初步里程碑，揭示了两种从众行为具有不同的内部机制。

Abstract: The conformity bias exhibited by large language models (LLMs) can pose a significant challenge to decision-making in LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS). While many prior studies have treated "conformity" simply as a matter of opinion change, this study introduces the social psychological distinction between informational conformity and normative conformity in order to understand LLM conformity at the mechanism level. Specifically, we design new tasks to distinguish between informational conformity, in which participants in a discussion are motivated to make accurate judgments, and normative conformity, in which participants are motivated to avoid conflict or gain acceptance within a group. We then conduct experiments based on these task settings. The experimental results show that, among the six LLMs evaluated, up to five exhibited tendencies toward not only informational conformity but also normative conformity. Furthermore, intriguingly, we demonstrate that by manipulating subtle aspects of the social context, it may be possible to control the target toward which a particular LLM directs its normative conformity. These findings suggest that decision-making in LLM-MAS may be vulnerable to manipulation by a small number of malicious users. In addition, through analysis of internal vectors associated with informational and normative conformity, we suggest that although both behaviors appear externally as the same form of "conformity," they may in fact be driven by distinct internal mechanisms. Taken together, these results may serve as an initial milestone toward understanding how "norms" are implemented in LLMs and how they influence group dynamics.

</details>


### [31] [Formally Verified Patent Analysis via Dependent Type Theory: Machine-Checkable Certificates from a Hybrid AI + Lean 4 Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18882)
*George Koomullil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于Lean 4交互式定理证明的专利分析形式化验证框架，将专利权利要求编码为有向无环图，通过机器验证的算法核心确保分析的数学正确性，为知识产权分析引入了可组合、可验证的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专利分析方法主要依赖人工专家分析（缓慢、不可扩展）或机器学习/自然语言处理方法（概率性、不透明、非组合性），缺乏形式化保障。作者旨在构建一个既能保证数学正确性又具备可扩展性的混合AI+Lean 4框架。

Method: 将专利权利要求编码为Lean 4中的有向无环图(DAG)，匹配强度建模为经过验证的完备格元素，通过证明正确的单调函数传播置信度分数。框架形式化了五个IP用例（专利-产品映射、自由实施权、权利要求解释敏感性、交叉权利要求一致性、等同原则），核心覆盖算法在Lean 4中完成机器验证，高层定理采用架构缓解策略（不信任生成器输出经内核检查和sorry-free公理审计）。

Result: 成功实现DAG覆盖核心(Algorithm 1b)的完全机器验证；结构引理、覆盖核心生成器和闭路径恒等式coverage = W_cov均在Lean 4中得到验证；通过合成内存模块权利要求案例研究展示了加权覆盖和解释敏感性分析的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架是首个将基于依赖类型论的交互式定理证明应用于知识产权分析的工作，为ML评分下游计算提供了数学正确性认证（正确性条件性依赖于ML层），开辟了形式化IP分析的新方向。未来工作包括对已裁决案例的验证。

Abstract: We present a formally verified framework for patent analysis as a hybrid AI + Lean 4 pipeline. The DAG-coverage core (Algorithm 1b) is fully machine-verified once bounded match scores are fixed. Freedom-to-operate, claim-construction sensitivity, cross-claim consistency, and doctrine-of-equivalents analyses are formalized at the specification level with kernel-checked candidate certificates. Existing patent-analysis approaches rely on manual expert analysis (slow, non-scalable) or ML/NLP methods (probabilistic, opaque, non-compositional). To our knowledge, this is the first framework that applies interactive theorem proving based on dependent type theory to intellectual property analysis. Claims are encoded as DAGs in Lean 4, match strengths as elements of a verified complete lattice, and confidence scores propagate through dependencies via proven-correct monotone functions. We formalize five IP use cases (patent-to-product mapping, freedom-to-operate, claim construction sensitivity, cross-claim consistency, doctrine of equivalents) via six algorithms. Structural lemmas, the coverage-core generator, and the closed-path identity coverage = W_cov are machine-verified in Lean 4. Higher-level theorems for the other use cases remain informal proof sketches, and their proof-generation functions are architecturally mitigated (untrusted generators whose outputs are kernel-checked and sorry-free axiom-audited). Guarantees are conditional on the ML layer: they certify mathematical correctness of computations downstream of ML scores, not the accuracy of the scores themselves. A case study on a synthetic memory-module claim demonstrates weighted coverage and construction-sensitivity analysis. Validation against adjudicated cases is future work.

</details>


### [32] [Integrating Anomaly Detection into Agentic AI for Proactive Risk Management in Human Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19538)
*Farbod Zorriassatine,Ahmad Lotfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将跌倒检测与预测重构为异常检测问题，并利用代理型AI系统来应对老年人群跌倒风险，以克服现有系统在现实复杂场景中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跌倒缓解系统（检测与预测）尚未成为跨护理路径和安全关键环境的通用解决方案。其主要局限性在于无法一致性地处理现实世界的复杂性，具体表现为上下文感知能力差、误报率高、环境噪声干扰以及数据稀缺。

Method: 提出一种概念性框架，将跌倒检测和预测表述为异常检测问题，并通过代理型AI（Agentic AI）系统加以解决。该框架采用协调有序的风险管理方法，通过动态选择相关工具并将其集成到自适应决策工作流中，替代了针对狭义场景定制的静态配置。

Result: 该视角能够实现对与风险增加相关的细微运动模式偏差的早期识别，这些偏差可能源于年龄相关衰退、疲劳或环境因素。本文构建了一个概念框架，展示了代理型AI在解决现有系统局限性方面的潜在价值。

Conclusion: 通过代理型AI系统实现的动态工具选择与自适应决策工作流，可以促进更有效的风险管理方法。尽管本文未涉及立即部署的技术要求，但该概念框架为解决人类活动中与运动相关的风险（特别是老年人跌倒问题）提供了新的思路和方向。

Abstract: Agentic AI, with goal-directed, proactive, and autonomous decision-making capabilities, offers a compelling opportunity to address movement-related risks in human activity, including the persistent hazard of falls among elderly populations. Despite numerous approaches to fall mitigation through fall prediction and detection, existing systems have not yet functioned as universal solutions across care pathways and safety-critical environments. This is largely due to limitations in consistently handling real-world complexity, particularly poor context awareness, high false alarm rates, environmental noise, and data scarcity. We argue that fall detection and fall prediction can usefully be formulated as anomaly detection problems and more effectively addressed through an agentic AI system. More broadly, this perspective enables the early identification of subtle deviations in movement patterns associated with increased risk, whether arising from age-related decline, fatigue, or environmental factors. While technical requirements for immediate deployment are beyond the scope of this paper, we propose a conceptual framework that highlights potential value. This framework promotes a well-orchestrated approach to risk management by dynamically selecting relevant tools and integrating them into adaptive decision-making workflows, rather than relying on static configurations tailored to narrowly defined scenarios.

</details>


### [33] [Error-free Training for MedMNIST Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18916)
*Bo Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“人工特化智能”概念，通过该机制分类模型可实现零错误训练，并在MedMNIST生物医学数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习分类模型在训练中难免出现错误并可能重复犯错，作者希望赋予模型“不再重复犯错”的能力，进而追求在训练集上达到完美表现。

Method: 提出Artificial Special Intelligence概念，构建一套使分类模型在训练过程中不产生错误的训练机制；将该框架应用于MedMNIST的18个生物医学数据集进行分类任务训练。

Result: 在18个MedMNIST数据集上，除3个存在双标签问题的数据集外，其余15个数据集均实现了训练至完美（零错误）的表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的Artificial Special Intelligence方法能够在合适的条件下使分类模型训练至零错误，证明了避免重复犯错、实现完美训练的可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a new concept called Artificial Special Intelligence by which Machine Learning models for the classification problem can be trained error-free, thus acquiring the capability of not making repeated mistakes. The method is applied to 18 MedMNIST biomedical datasets. Except for three datasets, which suffer from the double-labeling problem, all are trained to perfection.

</details>


### [34] [AblateCell: A Reproduce-then-Ablate Agent for Virtual Cell Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19606)
*Xue Xia,Chengkai Yao,Mingyu Tsoi,Xinjie Mao,Wenxuan Huang,Jiaqi Wei,Hao Wu,Cheng Tan,Lang Yu,Yuejin Yang,Siqi Sun,Zhangyang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AblateCell，一个针对虚拟细胞代码库的“复现-消融”智能体，解决了生物AI模型缺乏系统性消融实验的问题，显著提升了工作流成功率和关键组件识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI虚拟细胞研究中，系统性消融实验对于归因性能提升至关重要，但由于生物代码库标准化程度低且与特定领域数据紧密耦合，此类实验很少被执行。现有的编码智能体仅能生成代码，缺乏能够复现基线并严格验证组件重要性的验证器。

Method: 引入了AblateCell智能体，采用“先复现后消融”的闭环流程。首先，通过自动配置环境、解决依赖与数据问题及重跑官方评估，端到端地复现基线结果并生成可验证的产物。随后，生成隔离的代码库突变图谱，并在权衡性能影响与执行成本的奖励机制下，自适应选择实验进行闭环消融研究。

Result: 在三个单细胞扰动预测代码库（CPA、GEARS、BioLORD）上进行的评估显示，AblateCell实现了88.9%的端到端工作流成功率（比人类专家高29.9%），并在恢复真实关键组件方面达到了93.3%的准确率（比启发式方法高53.3%）。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了可扩展的、基于代码库的验证与归因能力，能够直接在生物代码库上严格验证并识别关键组件，填补了现有研究的空白。

Abstract: Systematic ablations are essential to attribute performance gains in AI Virtual Cells, yet they are rarely performed because biological repositories are under-standardized and tightly coupled to domain-specific data and formats. While recent coding agents can translate ideas into implementations, they typically stop at producing code and lack a verifier that can reproduce strong baselines and rigorously test which components truly matter. We introduce AblateCell, a reproduce-then-ablate agent for virtual cell repositories that closes this verification gap. AblateCell first reproduces reported baselines end-to-end by auto-configuring environments, resolving dependency and data issues, and rerunning official evaluations while emitting verifiable artifacts. It then conducts closed-loop ablation by generating a graph of isolated repository mutations and adaptively selecting experiments under a reward that trades off performance impact and execution cost. Evaluated on three single-cell perturbation prediction repositories (CPA, GEARS, BioLORD), AblateCell achieves 88.9% (+29.9% to human expert) end-to-end workflow success and 93.3% (+53.3% to heuristic) accuracy in recovering ground-truth critical components. These results enable scalable, repository-grounded verification and attribution directly on biological codebases.

</details>


### [35] [AutomationBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18934)
*Daniel Shepard,Robin Salimans*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AutomationBench，一个用于评估AI智能体跨应用程序工作流编排能力的基准测试，结果显示当前最佳前沿模型得分低于10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI软件自动化基准测试很少同时结合跨应用程序协调、自主API发现和策略遵守这三个方面，而实际业务工作流（如跨越CRM、收件箱、日历和消息平台）需要具备这三种能力，因此存在明显差距。

Method: 基于Zapier平台的真实工作流模式，构建了涵盖销售、营销、运营、支持、财务和人力资源领域的任务。智能体必须自行发现相关端点、遵循分层业务规则，并在包含无关甚至误导性记录的环境中导航。评分采用程序化且仅基于最终状态的方式：判断正确数据是否最终到达正确系统。

Result: 当前最佳前沿模型在AutomationBench上的得分低于10%，表明现有模型在满足企业实际需求的智能体能力方面仍存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: AutomationBench提供了一个具有挑战性且现实的衡量标准，能够准确反映当前模型相对于企业实际所需智能体能力的位置。

Abstract: Existing AI benchmarks for software automation rarely combine cross-application coordination, autonomous API discovery, and policy adherence. Real business workflows demand all three: a single task may span a CRM, inbox, calendar, and messaging platform - requiring the agent to find the right endpoints, follow a policy document, and write correct data to each system. To address this gap, we introduce AutomationBench, a benchmark for evaluating AI agents on cross-application workflow orchestration via REST APIs. Drawing on real workflow patterns from Zapier's platform, tasks span Sales, Marketing, Operations, Support, Finance, and HR domains. Agents must discover relevant endpoints themselves, follow layered business rules, and navigate environments with irrelevant and sometimes misleading records. Grading is programmatic and end-state only: whether the correct data ended up in the right systems. Even the best frontier models currently score below 10%. AutomationBench provides a challenging, realistic measure of where current models stand relative to the agentic capabilities businesses actually need.

</details>


### [36] [Personalized Benchmarking: Evaluating LLMs by Individual Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18943)
*Cristina Garbacea,Heran Wang,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前LLM基准测试使用平均偏好排名，忽略了个体用户偏好。本文发现个体排名与聚合排名严重偏离（Bradley-Terry相关性仅0.04），用户主题和风格特征可有效预测个性化排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在实际任务中的部署，评估其与人类偏好的对齐至关重要。现有基准测试通过平均所有用户偏好计算聚合评分，忽视了个体用户在不同语境下的差异化偏好，无法满足用户的个性化需求。

Method: 针对115名活跃Chatbot Arena用户，使用ELO评分和Bradley-Terry系数计算个性化模型排名；通过主题建模和风格分析研究用户查询特征与排名变化的关系；构建主题和风格特征组合来预测用户特定的模型排名。

Result: 个体排名与聚合排名严重偏离：Bradley-Terry系数平均相关性仅ρ=0.04（57%用户呈近零或负相关），ELO评分呈中等相关性（ρ=0.43）；用户在主题兴趣和沟通风格上呈现显著异质性；主题和风格特征的紧凑组合可有效预测用户特定的模型排名。

Conclusion: 聚合基准测试无法捕获大多数用户的个体偏好，提供了强有力的定量证据，强调开发根据个体用户偏好对LLM模型进行排名的个性化基准测试的重要性。

Abstract: With the rise in capabilities of large language models (LLMs) and their deployment in real-world tasks, evaluating LLM alignment with human preferences has become an important challenge. Current benchmarks average preferences across all users to compute aggregate ratings, overlooking individual user preferences when establishing model rankings. Since users have varying preferences in different contexts, we call for personalized LLM benchmarks that rank models according to individual needs. We compute personalized model rankings using ELO ratings and Bradley-Terry coefficients for 115 active Chatbot Arena users and analyze how user query characteristics (topics and writing style) relate to LLM ranking variations. We demonstrate that individual rankings of LLM models diverge dramatically from aggregate LLM rankings, with Bradley-Terry correlations averaging only $ρ= 0.04$ (57\% of users show near-zero or negative correlation) and ELO ratings showing moderate correlation ($ρ= 0.43$). Through topic modeling and style analysis, we find users exhibit substantial heterogeneity in topical interests and communication styles, influencing their model preferences. We further show that a compact combination of topic and style features provides a useful feature space for predicting user-specific model rankings. Our results provide strong quantitative evidence that aggregate benchmarks fail to capture individual preferences for most users, and highlight the importance of developing personalized benchmarks that rank LLM models according to individual user preferences.

</details>


### [37] [Reasoning Structure Matters for Safety Alignment of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18946)
*Yeonjun In,Wonjoong Kim,Sangwu Park,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AltTrain，一种通过显式改变推理结构来实现大推理模型安全对齐的简单有效方法，仅需1K样本的监督微调即可实现强安全性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRMs）在复杂推理任务上表现优异，但面对恶意用户查询时经常生成有害响应。现有安全风险的根本原因在于模型自身的推理结构存在问题，而非仅仅是训练数据或方法的不足。

Method: 提出AltTrain方法，显式改变LRMs的推理结构以实现有效安全对齐。该方法简单实用且具有泛化性，无需复杂的强化学习训练或奖励设计，仅采用监督微调（SFT）方式，使用轻量级的1K训练样本即可完成。

Result: 在多种LRM骨干网络和不同模型规模上的实验表明，AltTrain实现了强安全对齐效果，同时在推理、问答、摘要和多语言等多种设置中保持了稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过改变推理结构而非仅依赖传统对齐方法，可以高效实现LRMs的安全对齐。AltTrain证明了简单方法在解决复杂安全问题上的有效性，为未来模型安全研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks but often generate harmful responses to malicious user queries. This paper investigates the underlying cause of these safety risks and shows that the issue lies in the reasoning structure itself. Based on this insight, we claim that effective safety alignment can be achieved by altering the reasoning structure. We propose AltTrain, a simple yet effective post training method that explicitly alters the reasoning structure of LRMs. AltTrain is both practical and generalizable, requiring no complex reinforcement learning (RL) training or reward design, only supervised finetuning (SFT) with a lightweight 1K training examples. Experiments across LRM backbones and model sizes demonstrate strong safety alignment, along with robust generalization across reasoning, QA, summarization, and multilingual setting.

</details>


### [38] [DW-Bench: Benchmarking LLMs on Data Warehouse Graph Topology Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18964)
*Ahmed G. A. H Ahmed,C. Okan Sakar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DW-Bench基准，用于评估大语言模型在数据仓库模式上的图拓扑推理能力，发现工具增强方法显著优于静态方法但在复杂组合子任务上遇到瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试缺乏对大语言模型在数据仓库模式上进行图拓扑推理能力的评估，特别是未能同时整合外键（FK）和数据血缘边这两种关键的关系类型，因此需要一个新的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 引入DW-Bench基准，该基准包含五个模式、1,046个自动生成且可验证正确的问题，明确整合了外键和数据血缘边，专门用于评估LLM在图拓扑推理方面的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，工具增强的方法显著优于静态方法，但在处理困难的组合类型子任务时，性能会出现停滞，无法进一步提升。
