Conclusion: DW-Bench为评估LLM在数据仓库图拓扑推理方面提供了有效工具，揭示了当前方法在处理复杂组合推理任务时的局限性，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: This paper introduces DW-Bench, a new benchmark that evaluates large language models (LLMs) on graph-topology reasoning over data warehouse schemas, explicitly integrating both foreign-key (FK) and data-lineage edges. The benchmark comprises 1,046 automatically generated, verifiably correct questions across five schemas. Experiments show that tool-augmented methods substantially outperform static approaches but plateau on hard compositional subtypes.

</details>


### [39] [SAVOIR: Learning Social Savoir-Faire via Shapley-based Reward Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18982)
*Xiachong Feng,Yi Jiang,Xiaocheng Feng,Deyi Yin,Libo Qin,Yangfan Ye,Lei Huang,Weitao Ma,Yuxuan Gu,Chonghan Qin,Bing Qin,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAVOIR框架，利用期望效用和沙普利值解决语言智能体社会智能训练中的信用分配问题，在SOTOPIA基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会智能是语言智能体面临的基本挑战。现有强化学习方法采用语言模型直接分配回合级奖励，导致归因具有回顾性且缺乏理论基础，无法准确判断单个话语对多轮对话结果的贡献。

Method: 提出SAVOIR框架，基于合作博弈理论结合两个互补原则：1）期望效用将评估从回顾性归因转向前瞻性估值，捕捉话语对未来有利轨迹的战略潜力；2）沙普利值确保信用分配的公平性，具有效率性、对称性和边际性的公理保证。

Result: 在SOTOPIA基准上取得新的最优性能，7B模型达到或超越GPT-4o和Claude-3.5-Sonnet等专有模型。大型推理模型表现持续不佳，表明社会智能需要与分析推理不同的能力。

Conclusion: 基于博弈论理论的信用分配方法能有效提升语言智能体的社会智能，社会智能的本质可能不同于传统的分析推理能力。

Abstract: Social intelligence, the ability to navigate complex interpersonal interactions, presents a fundamental challenge for language agents. Training such agents via reinforcement learning requires solving the credit assignment problem: determining how individual utterances contribute to multi-turn dialogue outcomes. Existing approaches directly employ language models to distribute episode-level rewards, yielding attributions that are retrospective and lack theoretical grounding. We propose SAVOIR (ShApley Value fOr SocIal RL), a novel principled framework grounded in cooperative game theory. Our approach combines two complementary principles: expected utility shifts evaluation from retrospective attribution to prospective valuation, capturing an utterance's strategic potential for enabling favorable future trajectories; Shapley values ensure fair credit distribution with axiomatic guarantees of efficiency, symmetry, and marginality. Experiments on the SOTOPIA benchmark demonstrate that SAVOIR achieves new state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation settings, with our 7B model matching or exceeding proprietary models including GPT-4o and Claude-3.5-Sonnet. Notably, even large reasoning models consistently underperform, suggesting social intelligence requires qualitatively different capabilities than analytical reasoning.

</details>


### [40] [On Accelerating Grounded Code Development for Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19022)
*Santosh Ganji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源框架，通过为编程智能体提供对研究仓库和技术文档的即时访问，解决了专业科学和技术领域因缺乏最新领域知识而难以利用编程智能体的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础模型在材料科学、通信工程和生物工程等专业领域面临两大局限：一是缺乏最新的领域特定知识，二是推理能力有限；同时，领域专家通常缺乏微调大模型或持续嵌入新发现的资源，这阻碍了AI编程智能体的应用。

Method: 提出了一个框架，使编程智能体能够实时访问研究仓库和技术文档，实现上下文感知操作。提供了开源实现，包括用于文档上传的doc-search.dev平台和用于强制执行领域特定规则与工作流的zed-fork工具。

Result: 开发了开源工具套件，支持用户上传文档并为编程智能体提供即时知识访问能力，实现了领域特定规则的强制执行和工作流集成。

Conclusion: 该框架加速了编程智能体与专业科学和技术工作流的整合，为资源有限的领域专家提供了无需微调大模型即可利用AI编程智能体的可行方案。

Abstract: A major challenge for niche scientific and technical domains in leveraging coding agents is the lack of access to up-to-date, domain- specific knowledge. Foundational models often demonstrate limited reasoning capabilities in specialized fields and cannot inherently incorporate knowledge that evolves through ongoing research and experimentation. Materials scientists exploring novel compounds, communication engineers designing and evaluating new protocols, and bioengineering researchers conducting iterative experiments all face this limitation. These experts typically lack the resources to fine-tune large models or continuously embed new findings, creating a barrier to adopting AI-driven coding agents. To address this, we introduce a framework that gives coding agents instanta- neous access to research repositories and technical documentation, enabling real-time, context-aware operation. Our open-source im- plementation allows users to upload documents via doc-search.dev and includes zed-fork, which enforces domain-specific rules and workflows. Together, these tools accelerate the integration of coding agents into specialized scientific and technical workflows

</details>


### [41] [Plausible Reasoning and First-Order Plausible Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19036)
*David Billington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“似然逻辑”（Plausible Logic, PL）的一阶逻辑系统，能够满足17条似然推理原则中的大部分，并正确处理多个推理示例。该逻辑系统包含8种推理算法，用于处理不同的合理结论情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理系统在处理“可废止陈述”（即可能为真但偶尔为假的陈述）时，往往依赖概率或数值方法。作者希望构建一种无需数值、仅基于陈述本身的“似然推理”逻辑框架，以更好地模拟人类的常识推理过程。

Method: 作者首先总结了17条似然推理逻辑原则（其中14条为必要原则，3条为理想原则），并分析了多个重要的似然推理示例。随后，基于这些原则定义了一种一阶逻辑——似然逻辑（PL），该逻辑设计了8种推理算法来应对不同的推理场景。本文是作者即将出版的书籍《Plausible Reasoning and Plausible Logic》的精简版，详细证明略去。

Result: 所提出的似然逻辑（PL）满足全部14条必要原则和3条理想原则中的大部分（仅两条理想原则未满足），并且在所有考虑的似然推理示例中均能得出正确的结论。据作者所知，这是目前唯一满足这些要求的逻辑系统。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了一种无需数值、基于原则的似然推理逻辑系统PL，填补了现有逻辑系统在处理可废止陈述方面的空白。该工作为常识推理和人工智能中的不确定性推理提供了新的理论基础，详细内容将在作者即将出版的书籍中进一步展开。

Abstract: Defeasible statements are statements that are likely, or probable, or usually true, but may occasionally be false. Plausible reasoning makes conclusions from statements that are either facts or defeasible statements without using numbers. So there are no probabilities or suchlike involved. Seventeen principles of logics that do plausible reasoning are suggested and several important plausible reasoning examples are considered. There are 14 necessary principles and 3 desirable principles, one of which is not formally stated. A first-order logic, called Plausible Logic (PL), is defined that satisfies all but two of the desirable principles and reasons correctly with all the examples. As far as we are aware, this is the only such logic. PL has 8 reasoning algorithms because, from a given plausible reasoning situation, there are different sensible conclusions. This article is a condensation of my book `Plausible Reasoning and Plausible Logic' (PRPL), which is to be submitted. Each section of this article corresponds to a chapter in PRPL, and vice versa. The proofs of all the results are in PRPL, so they are omitted in this article.

</details>


### [42] [Learning Lifted Action Models from Unsupervised Visual Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19043)
*Kai Xi,Stephen Gould,Sylvie Thiébaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从状态图像序列中学习提升动作模型的深度学习框架，通过引入混合整数线性规划（MILP）来纠正预测错误，有效避免了预测坍塌和自我强化错误，实现了在无动作观察情况下的高效模型学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界领域中应用AI规划需要高效构建捕获动作前提条件和效果的动作模型。现有工作主要从高层状态描述或动作序列中学习模型，本文旨在解决更具挑战性的问题：在没有动作观察的情况下，仅从状态图像序列中学习提升动作模型（lifted action models）。

Method: 提出了一个深度学习框架，联合学习状态预测、动作预测和提升动作模型。核心创新是引入混合整数线性规划（MILP）机制：该MILP接收部分轨迹上的预测状态、动作和动作模型，求解在逻辑上一致且尽可能接近原始预测的状态、动作和动作模型。从MILP解中提取伪标签用于指导后续训练，防止预测坍塌和预测间的自我强化错误。

Result: 在多个领域的实验表明，集成基于MILP的纠正机制能够帮助模型逃离局部最优解，收敛到全局一致的解决方案。该方法成功实现了从图像状态序列无动作标签学习动作模型的目标。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过深度学习与MILP优化相结合的方法，可以有效解决从图像序列中无监督学习动作模型这一难题，为在缺乏动作标注的真实场景中应用AI规划提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Efficient construction of models capturing the preconditions and effects of actions is essential for applying AI planning in real-world domains. Extensive prior work has explored learning such models from high-level descriptions of state and/or action sequences. In this paper, we tackle a more challenging setting: learning lifted action models from sequences of state images, without action observation. We propose a deep learning framework that jointly learns state prediction, action prediction, and a lifted action model. We also introduce a mixed-integer linear program (MILP) to prevent prediction collapse and self-reinforcing errors among predictions. The MILP takes the predicted states, actions, and action model over a subset of traces and solves for logically consistent states, actions, and action model that are as close as possible to the original predictions. Pseudo-labels extracted from the MILP solution are then used to guide further training. Experiments across multiple domains show that integrating MILP-based correction helps the model escape local optima and converge toward globally consistent solutions.

</details>


### [43] [Reinforcement Learning Improves LLM Accuracy and Reasoning in Disease Classification from Radiology Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19060)
*Yishu Wei,Yi Lin,Adam Flanders,George Shih,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法：首先进行监督微调（SFT），随后利用群组相对策略优化（GRPO）进一步提升基于放射学报告的疾病分类性能，并增强了模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轻量级大语言模型在进行监督微调（SFT）以提高疾病分类准确率时，往往会导致模型推理能力的下降。为了在保证分类准确性的同时保持甚至增强推理能力，研究需要一种新的训练策略。

Method: 提出一种两阶段训练方法：第一阶段对轻量级LLM进行疾病标签的监督微调（SFT）；第二阶段应用群组相对策略优化（GRPO），在无需推理监督的情况下，通过优化准确性和输出格式来修正预测结果。

Result: 在三个放射科医生标注的数据集上，SFT阶段的表现优于基线模型；引入GRPO后，分类性能得到进一步提升，同时显著增强了推理的召回率和全面性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了SFT导致的推理能力退化问题，证明GRPO能够在缺乏推理监督的条件下，协同提升医学文本分类任务中的准确性与推理质量。

Abstract: Accurate disease classification from radiology reports is essential for many applications. While supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of lightweight LLMs improves accuracy, it can degrade reasoning. We propose a two-stage approach: SFT on disease labels followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to refine predictions by optimizing accuracy and format without reasoning supervision. Across three radiologist-annotated datasets, SFT outperformed baselines and GRPO further improved classification and enhanced reasoning recall and comprehensiveness.

</details>


### [44] [OLLM: Options-based Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19087)
*Shashank Sharma,Janina Hoffmann,Vinay Namboodiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了Options LLM (OLLM)，通过用一组学习到的选项替代标准LLM的单一token预测，显式建模生成多样性，在数学推理任务上显著提升性能和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM依赖温度或采样启发式方法来生成多样性，缺乏显式的变异性建模机制，且在强化学习对齐中存在样本效率低和常见错位问题（如语言切换或退化推理）。

Method: OLLM是一个轻量级插件架构，在预训练LLM的输出层前插入编码器和解码器两层，用离散潜变量索引下一token的选项集。仅训练1.56%的参数，将潜空间中的选项选择与下游策略分离。

Result: 在1.7B参数模型上，基于OpenMathReasoning训练并在OmniMath评估，SOTA LoRA基线峰值正确率为51%，而OLLM在最优潜变量选择下可达约70%。训练的紧凑潜空间策略显著提高了样本效率并减少了常见错位问题。

Conclusion: 选项化的下一token建模增强了数学推理的可控性、鲁棒性和效率，潜空间策略学习是LLM强化学习的有前景方向，对齐效果源于模型结构而非额外的KL散度或手工对齐损失。

Abstract: We introduce Options LLM (OLLM), a simple, general method that replaces the single next-token prediction of standard LLMs with a \textit{set of learned options} for the next token, indexed by a discrete latent variable. Instead of relying on temperature or sampling heuristics to induce diversity, OLLM models variation explicitly: a small latent space parametrizes multiple plausible next-token options which can be selected or searched by a downstream policy. Architecturally, OLLM is a lightweight "plug-in" that inserts two layers: an encoder and a decoder, before the output head, allowing almost any pretrained LLM to be converted with minimal additional parameters. We apply OLLM to a 1.7B-parameter backbone (only $1.56\%$ of parameters trainable) trained on OpenMathReasoning and evaluated on OmniMath. The SOTA LoRA-adapted baselines peak at $51\%$ final answer correctness, while OLLM's option set allows up to $\sim 70\%$ under optimal latent selection. We then train a compact policy in the latent space that emits latents to control generation. Operating in a low-dimensional option space makes reward optimization far more sample-efficient and substantially reduces common misalignments (e.g., language switching or degenerate reasoning), as the policy is constrained to options learned during SFT. Crucially, this alignment arises from model structure rather than additional KL or handcrafted alignment losses. Our results demonstrate that optionized next-token modeling enhances controllability, robustness, and efficiency in math reasoning, and highlight latent-space policy learning as a promising direction for reinforcement learning in LLMs.

</details>


### [45] [Towards Scalable Lifelong Knowledge Editing with Selective Knowledge Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19089)
*Dahyun Jung,Jaewook Lee,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LightEdit框架，通过检索相关信息并结合解码策略抑制原始知识概率，解决了大语言模型在终身知识编辑中存在的灾难性遗忘和训练成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要频繁更新知识以反映事实变化并减少幻觉。现有的参数编辑方法在序列编辑中面临灾难性遗忘导致的稳定性问题，而基于检索的方法虽能缓解此问题，但因训练成本高而难以跨数据集广泛应用。

Method: 提出了LightEdit框架：首先从检索信息中选择相关知识以有效修改查询；然后引入解码策略抑制模型原始知识概率，从而基于选定信息实现高效编辑。该方法避免了复杂的模型训练过程。

Result: 在ZSRE、Counterfact和RIPE基准测试上的广泛实验表明，LightEdit优于现有的终身知识编辑方法。同时，通过最小化训练成本，实现了具有成本效益的可扩展性，便于适应各种数据集。

Conclusion: LightEdit成功平衡了终身知识编辑中的编辑效果与可扩展性，为解决大语言模型知识更新的实际应用难题提供了高效、低成本的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) require frequent knowledge updates to reflect changing facts and mitigate hallucinations. To meet this demand, lifelong knowledge editing has emerged as a continual approach to modify specific pieces of knowledge without retraining the entire model. Existing parameter editing methods struggle with stability during sequential edits due to catastrophic forgetting. While retrieval-based approaches are proposed to alleviate this issue, their applicability remains limited across various datasets because of high training costs. To address these limitations and enhance scalability in lifelong settings, we propose LightEdit. Our framework first selects relevant knowledge from retrieved information to modify the query effectively. It then incorporates a decoding strategy to suppress the model's original knowledge probabilities, thereby enabling efficient edits based on the selected information. Extensive experiments on ZSRE, Counterfact, and RIPE benchmarks demonstrate that LightEdit outperforms existing lifelong knowledge editing methods. Furthermore, by minimizing training costs, LightEdit achieves cost-effective scalability, enabling easy adaptation to various datasets.

</details>


### [46] [Has Automated Essay Scoring Reached Sufficient Accuracy? Deriving Achievable QWK Ceilings from Classical Test Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19131)
*Masaki Uto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对自动作文评分（AES）评估中缺乏明确QWK理论上限和实用标准的问题，基于经典测量理论提出了两个数据集特定的QWK上限指标，并通过实验验证了这些上限能有效衡量现代AES模型的真实性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AES评估依赖于公共基准和二次加权Kappa（QWK）指标，但由于基准标签由人工评分员标注且不可避免地包含评分误差，目前尚不清楚理论上可达到的最高QWK值以及部署时所需的实际性能标准，缺乏对模型性能的合理评估基准。

Method: 基于经典测量理论中的信度概念，推导出两个数据集特定的QWK上限指标：1) 理论上限——在标签噪声下，能够完美预测潜在真实分数的理想AES模型所能达到的最大QWK值；2) 人类水平上限——当AES旨在替代单个人类评分员时，具有人类水平评分误差的AES模型可达到的QWK值。这些指标可从标准的双评分员基准数据中估计，无需额外标注。同时证明常用作参考的人类-人类QWK值可能低估真实的性能上限。

Result: 仿真实验验证了所提出上限指标的有效性。在真实基准数据集上的实验表明，这些上限指标能够有效阐明现代AES模型的当前性能水平和剩余改进空间，为AES模型评估提供了更准确的参考标准。

Conclusion: 本研究为AES领域提供了理论上有据可依的性能上限评估框架，解决了长期困扰该领域的评估基准不明确问题，使研究者和实践者能够更准确地理解模型性能、设定合理的研发目标，并为AES系统的实际部署提供了科学的性能标准参考。

Abstract: Automated essay scoring (AES) is commonly evaluated on public benchmarks using quadratic weighted kappa (QWK). However, because benchmark labels are assigned by human raters and inevitably contain scoring errors, it remains unclear both what QWK is theoretically attainable and what level is practically sufficient for deployment. We therefore derive two dataset-specific QWK ceilings based on the reliability concept in classical test theory, which can be estimated from standard two-rater benchmarks without additional annotation. The first is the theoretical ceiling: the maximum QWK that an ideal AES model that perfectly predicts latent true scores can achieve under label noise. The second is the human-like ceiling: the QWK attainable by an AES model with human-level scoring error, providing a practical target when AES is intended to replace a single human rater. We further show that human--human QWK, often used as a ceiling reference, can underestimate the true ceiling. Simulation experiments validate the proposed ceilings, and experiments on real benchmarks illustrate how they clarify the current performance and remaining headroom of modern AES models.

</details>


### [47] [Reasoning-Aware AIGC Detection via Alignment and Reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19172)
*Zhao Wang,Max Xiong,Jianxun Lian,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了REVEAL框架，通过生成可解释的推理链进行AIGC检测，在多基准测试中达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）的快速发展和广泛普及，对可靠的AI生成内容（AIGC）检测需求日益迫切，但模型不断演进使得检测任务依然极具挑战性。

Method: 构建了一个包含多领域、多LLM来源及多作者场景的综合数据集AIGC-text-bank；提出了REVEAL检测框架，采用两阶段训练策略：首先通过监督微调建立推理能力，随后利用强化学习提升准确性、逻辑一致性并减少幻觉，该框架在分类前会先生成可解释的推理链。

Result: 大量实验表明，REVEAL在多个基准测试中取得了最先进（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: REVEAL为AIGC检测提供了一个鲁棒且透明的解决方案，有效应对了模型演进带来的挑战。

Abstract: The rapid advancement and widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) have elevated the need for reliable AI-generated content (AIGC) detection, which remains challenging as models evolve. We introduce AIGC-text-bank, a comprehensive multi-domain dataset with diverse LLM sources and authorship scenarios, and propose REVEAL, a detection framework that generates interpretable reasoning chains before classification. Our approach uses a two-stage training strategy: supervised fine-tuning to establish reasoning capabilities, followed by reinforcement learning to improve accuracy, improve logical consistency, and reduce hallucinations. Extensive experiments show that REVEAL achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, offering a robust and transparent solution for AIGC detection. The project is open-source at https://aka.ms/reveal

</details>


### [48] [ClawNet: Human-Symbiotic Agent Network for Cross-User Autonomous Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19211)
*Zhiqin Yang,Zhenyuan Zhang,Xianzhang Jia,Jun Song,Wei Xue,Yonggang Zhang,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出人机共生智能体范式ClawNet，通过分层身份架构、作用域授权和行动级问责三大治理原语，实现跨用户AI智能体安全协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体框架仅服务于单一用户，缺乏跨用户协作的基础设施和治理机制。而人类生产力依赖于社会和组织关系中的协调、谈判和委托，因此需要将AI智能体从执行个人任务推进到代表人类进行协作的数字化阶段。

Method: 提出人机共生智能体范式，包含三大治理原语：(1)分层身份架构，将管理者智能体与多个特定情境的身份智能体分离，管理者智能体拥有全局知识但架构上与外部通信隔离；(2)作用域授权，强制执行每个身份的访问控制并将边界违规升级上报所有者；(3)行动级问责，记录每个操作对应的身份和授权以确保完全可审计性。基于此实现了ClawNet框架，通过中央编排器强制执行身份绑定和授权验证。

Result: 成功实例化ClawNet身份治理智能体协作框架，该框架通过中央编排器强制执行身份绑定和授权验证，使多个用户能够通过各自的智能体安全地进行协作。

Conclusion: AI智能体的下一个前沿不在于更强的个体能力，而在于人类协作关系的数字化。通过人机共生智能体范式及其三大治理原语，可以有效解决跨用户智能体协作中的身份治理和安全问题。

Abstract: Current AI agent frameworks have made remarkable progress in automating individual tasks, yet all existing systems serve a single user. Human productivity rests on the social and organizational relationships through which people coordinate, negotiate, and delegate. When agents move beyond performing tasks for one person to representing that person in collaboration with others, the infrastructure for cross-user agent collaboration is entirely absent, let alone the governance mechanisms needed to secure it. We argue that the next frontier for AI agents lies not in stronger individual capability, but in the digitization of human collaborative relationships. To this end, we propose a human-symbiotic agent paradigm. Each user owns a permanently bound agent system that collaborates on the owner's behalf, forming a network whose nodes are humans rather than agents. This paradigm rests on three governance primitives. A layered identity architecture separates a Manager Agent from multiple context-specific Identity Agents; the Manager Agent holds global knowledge but is architecturally isolated from external communication. Scoped authorization enforces per-identity access control and escalates boundary violations to the owner. Action-level accountability logs every operation against its owner's identity and authorization, ensuring full auditability. We instantiate this paradigm in ClawNet, an identity-governed agent collaboration framework that enforces identity binding and authorization verification through a central orchestrator, enabling multiple users to collaborate securely through their respective agents.

</details>


### [49] [UAF: A Unified Audio Front-end LLM for Full-Duplex Speech Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19221)
*Yadong Li,Guoxin Wu,Haiping Hou,Biye Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对全双工语音交互系统，本文提出了首个统一音频前端大语言模型（UAF），将VAD、TD、SR、ASR和QA等多种前端任务统一为自回归序列预测问题，在多项任务上取得领先性能并显著降低了响应延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端音频大语言模型（如GPT-4o）主要是半双工模式，且依赖VAD和轮流对话检测（TD）等独立、任务特定的前端组件，存在优化空间。作者观察到对于实现无缝、响应迅速的交互，优化语音前端与推进后端统一模型同样关键。

Method: 提出统一音频前端大语言模型（UAF），将多种音频前端任务（VAD、TD、SR、ASR、QA）重新表述为单一的自回归序列预测问题。模型以固定时长的流式音频块（如600ms）为输入，在起始阶段利用参考音频提示锚定目标说话人，并回归生成包含语义内容和系统级状态控制（如打断信号）的离散token。

Result: 实验表明，该模型在多项音频前端任务上达到领先性能，并在真实交互场景中显著提升了响应延迟和打断准确性。

Conclusion: 统一音频前端大语言模型（UAF）能够有效解决传统级联语音处理管道的局限性，通过统一建模多种前端任务，实现了性能提升和延迟优化，为全双工语音交互系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech interaction, as the most natural and intuitive mode of human communication, is driving artificial intelligence toward more human-like conversational systems. Traditional cascaded speech processing pipelines suffer from critical limitations, including accumulated latency, information loss, and error propagation across modules. To address these issues, recent efforts focus on the end-to-end audio large language models (LLMs) like GPT-4o, which primarily unify speech understanding and generation task. However, most of these models are inherently half-duplex, and rely on a suite of separate, task-specific front-end components, such as voice activity detection (VAD) and turn-taking detection (TD). In our development of speech assistant, we observed that optimizing the speech front-end is equally crucial as advancing the back-end unified model for achieving seamless, responsive interactions. To bridge this gap, we propose the first unified audio front-end LLM (UAF) tailored for full-duplex speech systems. Our model reformulates diverse audio front-end tasks into a single auto-regressive sequence prediction problem, including VAD, TD, speaker recognition (SR), automatic speech recognition (ASR) and question answer (QA). It takes streaming fixed-duration audio chunk (e.g., 600 ms) as input, leverages a reference audio prompt to anchor the target speaker at the beginning, and regressively generates discrete tokens encoding both semantic content and system-level state controls (e.g., interruption signals). Experiments demonstrate that our model achieves leading performance across multiple audio front-end tasks and significantly enhances response latency and interruption accuracy in real-world interaction scenarios.

</details>


### [50] [Industrial Surface Defect Detection via Diffusion Generation and Asymmetric Student-Teacher Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19240)
*Shuo Feng,Runlin Zhou,Yuyang Li,Guangcan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）与不对称师生网络的无监督缺陷检测方法。通过DDPM生成高质量缺陷样本解决数据稀缺问题，并利用师生网络放大异常区域差异，在MVTecAD数据集上实现了图像级AUROC 98.4%和像素级AUROC 98.3%的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业表面缺陷检测面临三大核心挑战：缺陷样本有限、数据呈严重长尾分布、复杂背景下微小缺陷难以精确定位。现有方法在这些方面表现不佳，亟需一种能够有效利用有限数据并实现精确定位的无监督检测方法。

Method: 该方法包含两个层面：数据层面，仅在正常样本上训练DDPM，通过引入恒定方差高斯扰动和基于Perlin噪声的掩码，生成高保真且物理一致的缺陷样本及其像素级标注；模型层面，构建不对称双流网络，教师网络提供稳定的正常特征表示，学生网络重建正常模式并放大正常与异常区域的差异，采用余弦相似度损失和像素级分割监督的联合优化策略实现缺陷精确定位。

Result: 在MVTecAD数据集上，该方法实现了图像级AUROC 98.4%和像素级AUROC 98.3%，显著优于现有的无监督方法和主流深度学习方法，证明了其在不依赖大量真实缺陷样本的情况下仍能实现准确鲁棒的缺陷检测和定位。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了工业缺陷检测中的数据稀缺问题，无需大量真实缺陷样本即可实现高精度的缺陷检测与像素级定位，为工业场景下的缺陷检测提供了一种高效、鲁棒的无监督解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial surface defect detection often suffers from limited defect samples, severe long-tailed distributions, and difficulties in accurately localizing subtle defects under complex backgrounds. To address these challenges, this paper proposes an unsupervised defect detection method that integrates a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) with an asymmetric teacher-student architecture. First, at the data level, the DDPM is trained solely on normal samples. By introducing constant-variance Gaussian perturbations and Perlin noise-based masks, high-fidelity and physically consistent defect samples along with pixel-level annotations are generated, effectively alleviating the data scarcity problem. Second, at the model level, an asymmetric dual-stream network is constructed. The teacher network provides stable representations of normal features, while the student network reconstructs normal patterns and amplifies discrepancies between normal and anomalous regions. Finally, a joint optimization strategy combining cosine similarity loss and pixel-wise segmentation supervision is adopted to achieve precise localization of subtle defects. Experimental results on the MVTecAD dataset show that the proposed method achieves 98.4\% image-level AUROC and 98.3\% pixel-level AUROC, significantly outperforming existing unsupervised and mainstream deep learning methods. The proposed approach does not require large amounts of real defect samples and enables accurate and robust industrial defect detection and localization.
  \keywords{Industrial defect detection \and diffusion models \and data generation \and teacher-student architecture \and pixel-level localization}

</details>


### [51] [Do Agents Dream of Root Shells? Partial-Credit Evaluation of LLM Agents in Capture The Flag Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19354)
*Ali Al-Kaswan,Maksim Plotnikov,Maxim Hájek,Roland Vízner,Arie van Deursen,Maliheh Izadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DeepRed，一个用于在真实CTF挑战中评估LLM智能体的开源基准测试。通过对10个商业LLM的测试发现，当前最强的模型平均检查点完成率仅为35%，表明LLM在自主网络安全攻击任务中能力仍然有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）智能体被越来越多地提议用于自主网络安全任务，但它们在现实攻击环境中的能力仍不明确。现有的评估方法往往过于简单，缺乏对真实复杂场景的模拟能力，因此需要建立一个更真实、更细粒度的评估基准。

Method: 提出DeepRed基准测试框架：1) 将智能体置于配备终端工具和可选网络搜索的Kali攻击者环境中，通过私有网络连接目标挑战；2) 引入基于公开writeups推导出的挑战特定检查点的部分评分方法，替代简单的二元解决/未解决结果；3) 开发自动化的“总结-判断”标注流水线，用于从日志中分配检查点完成情况。

Result: 在10个基于虚拟机的CTF挑战上对10个商业可用的LLM进行了基准测试。结果显示当前智能体能力有限：最佳模型平均检查点完成率仅为35%。模型在常见挑战类型上表现最强，但在需要非标准发现和长时域适应的任务上表现最弱。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在真实网络安全攻击场景中的能力仍然不足，特别是在处理需要非常规发现和长期规划的任务时存在显著短板，这为未来研究指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed for autonomous cybersecurity tasks, but their capabilities in realistic offensive settings remain poorly understood. We present DeepRed, an open-source benchmark for evaluating LLM-based agents on realistic Capture The Flag (CTF) challenges in isolated virtualized environments. DeepRed places an agent in a Kali attacker environment with terminal tools and optional web search, connected over a private network to a target challenge, and records full execution traces for analysis. To move beyond binary solved/unsolved outcomes, we introduce a partial-credit scoring method based on challenge-specific checkpoints derived from public writeups, together with an automated summarise-then-judge labelling pipeline for assigning checkpoint completion from logs. Using DeepRed, we benchmark ten commercially accessible LLMs on ten VM-based CTF challenges spanning different challenge categories. The results indicate that current agents remain limited: the best model achieves only 35% average checkpoint completion, performing strongest on common challenge types and weakest on tasks requiring non-standard discovery and longer-horizon adaptation.

</details>


### [52] [Towards Energy Impact on AI-Powered 6G IoT Networks: Centralized vs. Decentralized](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19377)
*Anjie Qiu,Donglin Wang,Sanket Partani,Andreas Weinand,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在德国铁路基础设施中部署测试平台，通过对比分析发现分布式机器学习架构在保持约90%预测准确率的同时，可降低高达70%的电力消耗，证明了其在物联网中提升能效的巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着第六代（6G）技术的发展，物联网网络中的机器学习应用面临新的能耗挑战。由于模型训练和数据传输是能耗的主要来源，优化这些过程对于设计可持续系统至关重要。

Method: 首先对集中式和分布式架构进行了能耗模型分析；随后在德国铁路基础设施中部署了一个测试平台，利用传感器数据进行基于机器学习的预测性维护；最后对分布式学习与集中式学习（CL）架构进行了对比分析。

Result: 对比分析表明，分布式模型在维持具有竞争力的预测准确率（约90%）的同时，能够将总电力消耗降低高达70%。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了分布式机器学习在现实物联网部署中提升能效的潜力，特别是通过减少与传输相关的能耗成本来实现。

Abstract: The emergence of sixth-generation (6G) technologies has introduced new challenges and opportunities for machine learning (ML) applications in Internet of Things (IoT) networks, particularly concerning energy efficiency. As model training and data transmission contribute significantly to energy consumption, optimizing these processes has become critical for sustainable system design. This study first conduct analysis on the energy consumption model for both centralized and decentralized architecture and then presents a testbed deployed within the German railway infrastructure, leveraging sensor data for ML-based predictive maintenance. A comparative analysis of distributed versus Centralized Learning (CL) architectures reveals that distributed models maintain competitive predictive accuracy (~90%) while reducing overall electricity consumption by up to 70%. These findings underscore the potential of distributed ML to improve energy efficiency in real-world IoT deployments, particularly by mitigating transmission-related energy costs.

</details>


### [53] [GRASPrune: Global Gating for Budgeted Structured Pruning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19398)
*Ziyang Wang,Jiangfeng Xiao,Chuan Xiao,Ruoxiang Li,Rui Mao,Jianbin Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了GRASPrune，一种结构化剪枝框架，通过联合修剪FFN通道和KV头组，在LLaMA-2-7B上移除50%参数，保持了竞争力的性能且无需微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在服务部署时面临高昂成本，主要源于模型参数、注意力计算和KV缓存带来的巨大内存和延迟开销。

Method: 提出GRASPrune框架，使用投影直通估计器学习轻量级门控分数，在训练的每一步强制执行硬掩码以满足全局预算，同时冻结主干权重。剪枝后校准缩放因子以缓解尺度不匹配，并将其折叠入剪枝后的权重。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B上移除50%参数后，WikiText-2困惑度为12.18，在五个基准测试上保持竞争力的平均零样本准确率。该过程仅需在单张NVIDIA A100 80GB GPU上使用512个无标签校准序列训练四个epoch，无需全模型微调。

Conclusion: GRASPrune证明了通过联合剪枝和缩放因子校准，可以在显著降低模型大小的同时保持良好性能，为LLM的高效部署提供了一种无需全量微调的有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are expensive to serve because model parameters, attention computation, and KV caches impose substantial memory and latency costs. We present GRASPrune, a structured pruning framework applied after pretraining that jointly prunes FFN channels and KV head groups under a single global budget. Instead of learning importance scores without constraints and applying the budget only after training, GRASPrune learns lightweight gate scores with a projected straight-through estimator that enforces a hard mask satisfying the budget at every step while keeping the backbone weights frozen. After the mask is fixed, we calibrate scaling factors on the retained units to mitigate scale mismatch caused by pruning, and fold these factors into the pruned weights to obtain a smaller dense checkpoint with no extra parameters at inference. On LLaMA-2-7B, GRASPrune removes 50% of parameters and achieves 12.18 perplexity on WikiText-2 while maintaining competitive average zero-shot accuracy on five benchmarks, using four epochs on 512 unlabeled calibration sequences on a single NVIDIA A100 80GB GPU without any full model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [54] [Four-Axis Decision Alignment for Long-Horizon Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19457)
*Vasundra Srininvasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于评估长周期企业决策智能体的四维正交对齐框架，包括事实精确度（FRP）、推理连贯性（RCS）、合规重构（CRR）和校准弃权（CAR），并在LongHorizon-Bench基准上验证了该框架能够揭示单一准确率指标所隐藏的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的长周期企业决策智能体（如贷款审批、理赔裁决等）在记忆丢失、多步推理和监管约束下面临高风险决策挑战。现有评估方法仅报告单一任务成功标量，混淆了不同的失败模式，无法判断智能体是否符合部署环境所需的对齐标准。

Method: 提出长周期决策行为可分解为四个正交对齐轴：事实精确度（FRP）、推理连贯性（RCS）、合规重构（CRR）和校准弃权（CAR）。构建了一个受控基准LongHorizon-Bench，涵盖贷款资格审查和保险理赔裁决，使用确定性真值构建，并在六种记忆架构上进行实验验证。

Result: 发现了聚合准确率无法看到的结构性问题：检索在事实精确度上崩溃；模式锚定架构需要支付脚手架税；带有事实保留提示的简单摘要成为FRP、RCS、EDA和CRR的强基线；所有六种架构在每个案例上都做出决策，暴露了一个领域尚未针对的决策对齐轴。数据还推翻了预先注册的预测（摘要会失败于事实回忆），这种轴级别的逆转是聚合准确率会隐藏的。

Conclusion: 机构对齐（监管重构）和决策对齐（校准弃权）在对齐文献中被低估，但在决策离开实验室环境后变得至关重要。该框架可通过两个步骤迁移到任何受监管决策领域：构建事实模式，校准CRR审计提示。

Abstract: Long-horizon enterprise agents make high-stakes decisions (loan underwriting, claims adjudication, clinical review, prior authorization) under lossy memory, multi-step reasoning, and binding regulatory constraints. Current evaluation reports a single task-success scalar that conflates distinct failure modes and hides whether an agent is aligned with the standards its deployment environment requires. We propose that long-horizon decision behavior decomposes into four orthogonal alignment axes, each independently measurable and failable: factual precision (FRP), reasoning coherence (RCS), compliance reconstruction (CRR), and calibrated abstention (CAR). CRR is a novel regulatory-grounded axis; CAR is a measurement axis separating coverage from accuracy. We exercise the decomposition on a controlled benchmark (LongHorizon-Bench) covering loan qualification and insurance claims adjudication with deterministic ground-truth construction. Running six memory architectures, we find structure aggregate accuracy cannot see: retrieval collapses on factual precision; schema-anchored architectures pay a scaffolding tax; plain summarization under a fact-preservation prompt is a strong baseline on FRP, RCS, EDA, and CRR; and all six architectures commit on every case, exposing a decisional-alignment axis the field has not targeted. The decomposition also surfaced a pre-registered prediction of our own, that summarization would fail factual recall, which the data reversed at large magnitude, an axis-level reversal aggregate accuracy would have hidden. Institutional alignment (regulatory reconstruction) and decisional alignment (calibrated abstention) are under-represented in the alignment literature and become load-bearing once decisions leave the laboratory. The framework transfers to any regulated decisioning domain via two steps: build a fact schema, and calibrate the CRR auditor prompt.

</details>


### [55] [Do LLMs Game Formalization? Evaluating Faithfulness in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19459)
*Kyuhee Kim,Auguste Poiroux,Antoine Bosselut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现了形式化验证中的“弄虚作假”现象：尽管编译成功率高达87-99%，模型仍可能通过捏造公理或错误翻译前提来生成不忠实的证明。高编译率/准确率不等同于忠实推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证能保证证明的有效性，但无法保证形式化的忠实度。在自然语言逻辑推理任务中，模型从零构建公理系统，缺乏库约束，这使得有效证明与忠实翻译之间的差距尤为突出。本文旨在探究前沿模型在生成Lean 4证明时是否利用这一差距进行“形式化博弈”（formalization gaming），即生成编译通过但不忠实的证明。

Method: 研究评估了GPT-5和DeepSeek-R1在303个一阶逻辑问题（203个来自FOLIO，100个来自Multi-LogiEval）上的表现。方法对比了两种生成模式：统一生成模式与将形式化和证明阶段分离的两阶段流水线。研究分析了模型的编译成功率、失败报告行为，并通过跨阶段比较检测不忠实行为。

Result: 实验显示编译成功率达87-99%。在统一生成模式下，未发现系统性博弈证据：模型倾向于报告失败而非强行生成证明。但在两阶段流水线中发现了两种不同的不忠实模式：GPT-5在证明生成阶段捏造公理（反应性回退行为，可通过跨阶段比较检测）；DeepSeek-R1在形式化阶段错误翻译前提（产生内部一致但实际错误的输出，完全规避检测）。

Conclusion: 研究得出重要警示：高编译率或高准确率不应等同于忠实推理。不同模型展现出截然不同的不忠实策略，其中DeepSeek-R1的错误翻译模式更具隐蔽性，难以被现有方法检测，这对形式化验证的可靠性评估提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Formal verification guarantees proof validity but not formalization faithfulness. For natural-language logical reasoning, where models construct axiom systems from scratch without library constraints, this gap between valid proofs and faithful translations is especially acute. We investigate whether frontier models exploit this gap when generating Lean 4 proofs, a behavior we term formalization gaming.
  We evaluate GPT-5 and DeepSeek-R1 on 303 first-order logic problems (203 from FOLIO, 100 from Multi-LogiEval), comparing unified generation against a two-stage pipeline that separates formalization from proving. Despite compilation rates of 87-99%, we find no evidence of systematic gaming in unified generation: models prefer reporting failure over forcing proofs, even under prompting designed to encourage it. However, unfaithfulness that evades our detection signals may still occur. The two-stage pipeline reveals two distinct modes of unfaithfulness: GPT-5 fabricates axioms during proof generation, a reactive fallback detectable via cross-stage comparison, while DeepSeek-R1 mistranslates premises during formalization, producing internally consistent outputs that evade detection entirely. These findings show that high compilation rates or accuracies should not be equated with faithful reasoning. Code and data are available at https://github.com/koreankiwi99/formalization-gaming.

</details>


### [56] [CoDA: Towards Effective Cross-domain Knowledge Transfer via CoT-guided Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19488)
*Jianzhi Yan,Le Liu,Buzhou Tang,Yang Xiang,Dongning Sun,Zhiming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoDA方法，通过轻量级适配器干预中间隐藏状态，结合特征蒸馏和分布匹配来解决跨领域逻辑推理中的领域迁移问题，在缺乏领域内样本的场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于上下文学习的方法依赖高质量领域内样本，但在专业稀缺领域（如低资源科学学科、新兴生物医学领域或小众法律管辖区）这些样本难以获取。虽然已有研究尝试使用跨领域样本作为替代，但由于源域和目标域之间存在显著的领域偏移，模型难以有效识别和利用潜在的共享推理模式，导致性能提升有限。

Method: 提出CoDA（Cross-Domain Adapter），采用轻量级适配器直接干预模型的中间隐藏状态。该方法结合两个关键技术：（1）基于特征的思维链（CoT）增强参考表示蒸馏；（2）使用最大均值差异（MMD）进行核化分布匹配，从而对齐源域和目标域的潜在推理表示，缓解领域偏移问题。

Result: 在多个模型家族和多个逻辑推理任务上进行了广泛实验，结果表明CoDA显著优于之前的最先进基线方法，取得了大幅度的性能提升。

Conclusion: CoDA成功解决了跨领域逻辑推理中领域偏移的核心挑战，通过潜在表示对齐实现了知识的有效迁移，为专业稀缺领域中的逻辑推理任务提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved substantial advances in logical reasoning, yet they continue to lag behind human-level performance. In-context learning provides a viable solution that boosts the model's performance via prompting its input with expert-curated, in-domain exemplars. However, in many real-world, expertise-scarce domains, such as low-resource scientific disciplines, emerging biomedical subfields, or niche legal jurisdictions, such high-quality in-domain demonstrations are inherently limited or entirely unavailable, thereby constraining the general applicability of these approaches. To mitigate this limitation, recent efforts have explored the retrieval of cross-domain samples as surrogate in-context demonstrations. Nevertheless, the resulting gains remain modest. This is largely attributable to the pronounced domain shift between source and target distributions, which impedes the model's ability to effectively identify and exploit underlying shared structures or latent reasoning patterns. Consequently, when relying solely on raw textual prompting, LLMs struggle to abstract and transfer such cross-domain knowledge in a robust and systematic manner. To address these issues, we propose CoDA, which employs a lightweight adapter to directly intervene in the intermediate hidden states. By combining feature-based distillation of CoT-enriched reference representations with Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) for kernelized distribution matching, our method aligns the latent reasoning representation of the source and target domains. Extensive experimental results on multiple logical reasoning tasks across various model families validate the efficacy of CoDA by significantly outperforming the previous state-of-the-art baselines by a large margin.

</details>


### [57] [From Experience to Skill: Multi-Agent Generative Engine Optimization via Reusable Strategy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19516)
*Beining Wu,Fuyou Mao,Jiong Lin,Cheng Yang,Jiaxuan Lu,Yifu Guo,Siyu Zhang,Yifan Wu,Ying Huang,Fu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGEO框架，将生成式引擎优化（GEO）重构为策略学习问题，通过多智能体协作与策略蒸馏实现跨任务、跨引擎的优化经验积累，在可见性与引用保真度上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GEO方法对每个优化实例进行孤立处理，无法在不同任务和引擎间积累或迁移有效策略，缺乏系统性的策略学习能力。

Method: 提出MAGEO多智能体框架，包含协调规划、编辑与保真度评估的执行层，并将验证有效的编辑模式逐步蒸馏为可复用的引擎特定优化技能；引入双分支评估协议实现内容编辑的因果归因，提出DSV-CF双轴度量统一语义可见性与归因准确性；发布多场景多引擎基准MSME-GEO-Bench。

Result: 在三个主流生成式引擎上的实验表明，MAGEO在可见性与引用保真度方面显著优于启发式基线，消融实验证实引擎特定偏好建模与策略复用是性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: MAGEO为可信赖的GEO提供了一种可扩展的学习驱动范式，展示了通过策略学习与跨引擎迁移实现生成式引擎优化的有效性。

Abstract: Generative engines (GEs) are reshaping information access by replacing ranked links with citation-grounded answers, yet current Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) methods optimize each instance in isolation, unable to accumulate or transfer effective strategies across tasks and engines. We reframe GEO as a strategy learning problem and propose MAGEO, a multi-agent framework in which coordinated planning, editing, and fidelity-aware evaluation serve as the execution layer, while validated editing patterns are progressively distilled into reusable, engine-specific optimization skills. To enable controlled assessment, we introduce a Twin Branch Evaluation Protocol for causal attribution of content edits and DSV-CF, a dual-axis metric that unifies semantic visibility with attribution accuracy. We further release MSME-GEO-Bench, a multi-scenario, multi-engine benchmark grounded in real-world queries. Experiments on three mainstream engines show that MAGEO substantially outperforms heuristic baselines in both visibility and citation fidelity, with ablations confirming that engine-specific preference modeling and strategy reuse are central to these gains, suggesting a scalable learning-driven paradigm for trustworthy GEO. Code is available at https://github.com/Wu-beining/MAGEO

</details>


### [58] [SimDiff: Depth Pruning via Similarity and Difference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19520)
*Yuli Chen,Shuhao Zhang,Fanshen Meng,Bo Cheng,Jiale Han,Qiang Tong,Xiulei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SimDiff方法，通过联合评估表示相似性和变换差异来改进LLM的深度剪枝，解决了单一启发式方法性能不稳定的问题，在保持91%以上性能的同时实现了1.49倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度剪枝方法仅依赖余弦距离这一单一启发式指标来识别冗余层，导致在不同架构上表现出不可预测的性能，甚至出现灾难性崩溃，缺乏可靠的层重要性评估标准。

Method: 提出SimDiff层重要性准则，从两个正交视角联合评估层：表示相似性和变换差异。使用两种不同度量量化差异：MSSD（对异常值敏感，识别做出决定性修正的层）和MASD（鲁棒地测量层的平均贡献）。

Result: 在0.5B到13B参数的多个模型上验证，SimDiff显著优于最先进基线。LLaMA2-7B在25%剪枝率下保持超过91%性能；LLaMA3.1-8B剪枝12层时实现最高1.49倍推理加速；剪枝模型可通过最小微调有效恢复。

Conclusion: SimDiff通过双视角评估成功解决了单一启发式方法的不稳定性问题，为大语言模型的深度剪枝提供了更可靠的层重要性标准，在性能保持和推理加速方面取得显著成效。

Abstract: Depth pruning improves the deployment efficiency of large language models (LLMs) by identifying and removing redundant layers. A widely accepted standard for this identification process is to measure the similarity between layers using cosine distance. However, we find that methods relying solely on this one-dimensional heuristic can exhibit unpredictable performance and even catastrophic collapse across different architectures. To address this issue, we propose SimDiff, a novel layer importance criterion that jointly evaluates layers from two orthogonal perspectives: representational similarity and transformation difference. The difference is quantified using two distinct metrics: MSSD, which is sensitive to outliers and identifies layers that make decisive corrections, and MASD, which robustly measures a layer's average contribution. Extensive experiments on multiple models ranging from 0.5B to 13B parameters demonstrate that SimDiff significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across various pruning ratios. Notably, our method retains over 91% of LLaMA2-7B's performance at a 25% pruning ratio and achieves up to a 1.49x inference speedup when pruning 12 layers on LLaMA3.1-8B. We also show that pruned models can be effectively recovered with minimal fine-tuning.

</details>


### [59] [Revac: A Social Deduction Reasoning Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19523)
*Mihir Shriniwas Arya,Avinash Anish,Aditya Ranjan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在MindGames Arena竞赛社会推理赛道中获得第一名的AI智能体Revac-8，其通过整合基于记忆的玩家画像、社交图谱分析和动态语气选择，展示了结构化记忆和自适应交流在高风险社会博弈环境中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会推理游戏（如Mafia）为AI带来了独特挑战：玩家需要在不确定性下进行推理，解读不完整且故意误导的信息，评估类人沟通，并做出战略性的淘汰决策。与确定性棋盘游戏不同，Mafia的成功不依赖于完美信息或暴力搜索，而是取决于在存在欺骗情况下的推理、记忆和适应能力。

Method: 设计并开发了名为Revac-8的AI智能体，其架构从一个简单的两阶段推理系统演变为多模块架构。该架构整合了三大核心组件：1）基于记忆的玩家画像；2）针对指控与辩护的社交图谱分析；3）用于沟通的动态语气选择。

Result: Revac-8智能体在MindGames Arena竞赛的社会推理赛道中获得第一名，展示了其在复杂社会博弈环境中的卓越表现。实验结果突显了结构化记忆和自适应通信模块对于在高风险社会环境中取得优异表现的重要性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了结构化记忆和自适应通信机制对于AI在社会推理游戏中的成功至关重要。通过整合记忆建模、社交关系分析和动态沟通策略，AI能够有效应对欺骗性环境下的复杂决策问题，为社会推理AI的发展提供了有价值的架构设计经验。

Abstract: Social deduction games such as Mafia present a unique AI challenge: players must reason under uncertainty, interpret incomplete and intentionally misleading information, evaluate human-like communication, and make strategic elimination decisions. Unlike deterministic board games, success in Mafia depends not on perfect information or brute-force search, but on inference, memory, and adaptability in the presence of deception. This work presents the design and evaluation of Revac-8, an AI agent developed for the Social Deduction track of the MindGames Arena competition, where it achieved first place. The final agent evolved from a simple two-stage reasoning system into a multi-module architecture that integrates memory-based player profiling, social-graph analysis of accusations and defenses, and dynamic tone selection for communication. These results highlight the importance of structured memory and adaptive communication for achieving strong performance in high-stakes social environments.

</details>


### [60] [DT2IT-MRM: Debiased Preference Construction and Iterative Training for Multimodal Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19544)
*Zhihong Zhang,Jie Zhao,Xiaojian Huang,Jin Xu,Zhuodong Luo,Xin Liu,Jiansheng Wei,Xuejin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DT2IT-MRM框架，通过去偏好的构建流程、T2I偏好数据重制和迭代训练方法，解决了现有多模态偏好数据集的三大挑战，在三个主要基准上取得了新的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态偏好数据集面临三个关键挑战：偏好强度缺乏细粒度、文本风格偏差、以及偏好信号不可靠；同时现有开源数据集存在大量噪声且缺乏有效的规模化整理方法来提升数据质量。

Method: 提出DT2IT-MRM框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)去偏好的构建流程（Debiased preference construction pipeline）；(2)文本到图像（T2I）偏好数据的新型重制方法；(3)迭代训练框架（Iterative Training framework），用于整理现有多模态偏好数据集以进行多模态奖励建模。

Result: DT2IT-MRM在三个主要基准测试上取得了新的最先进（state-of-the-art）整体性能：VL-RewardBench、Multimodal RewardBench和MM-RLHF-RewardBench。

Conclusion: 通过整合去偏差的偏好构建、T2I数据重制和迭代训练策略，DT2IT-MRM有效提升了多模态奖励模型的训练数据质量，证明了高质量偏好数据对MRM性能的关键作用，为多模态大语言模型与人类偏好对齐提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal reward models (MRMs) play a crucial role in aligning Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) with human preferences. Training a good MRM requires high-quality multimodal preference data. However, existing preference datasets face three key challenges: lack of granularity in preference strength, textual style bias, and unreliable preference signals. Besides, existing open-source multimodal preference datasets suffer from substantial noise, yet there is a lack of effective and scalable curation methods to enhance their quality. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{DT2IT-MRM}, which integrates a \textbf{D}ebiased preference construction pipeline, a novel reformulation of text-to-image (\textbf{T2I}) preference data, and an \textbf{I}terative \textbf{T}raining framework that curates existing multimodal preference datasets for \textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{R}eward \textbf{M}odeling. Our experimental results show that DT2IT-MRM achieves new \textbf{state-of-the-art} overall performance on three major benchmarks: VL-RewardBench, Multimodal RewardBench, and MM-RLHF-RewardBench.

</details>


### [61] [Enhancing Construction Worker Safety in Extreme Heat: A Machine Learning Approach Utilizing Wearable Technology for Predictive Health Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19559)
*Syed Sajid Ullah,Amir Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于注意力机制的LSTM模型，通过智能手表数据预测建筑工人热应激，测试准确率达95.40%，F1分数为0.982，为建筑安全管理系统提供了可靠的预测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑工人极易受到热应激威胁，但现有研究缺乏能将实时生理数据转化为可操作安全智能的工具，亟需开发高精度的实时预测方法。

Method: 采集19名沙特阿拉伯建筑工人佩戴Garmin Vivosmart 5智能手表的心率、心率变异性（HRV）和血氧饱和度等生理指标，对比开发了基线LSTM网络与基于注意力机制的LSTM模型进行热应激预测。

Result: 基于注意力的LSTM模型表现优于基线模型，测试准确率达到95.40%，Precision、Recall和F1分数均为0.982，显著减少了假阳性和假阴性数量。

Conclusion: 该模型不仅具有优异的预测性能，还具备良好的可解释性，适合集成到物联网安全系统和BIM仪表板中，推动了建筑行业主动式、信息驱动的安全管理发展。

Abstract: Construction workers are highly vulnerable to heat stress, yet tools that translate real-time physiological data into actionable safety intelligence remain scarce. This study addresses this gap by developing and evaluating deep learning models, specifically a baseline Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network and an attention-based LSTM, to predict heat stress among 19 workers in Saudi Arabia. Using Garmin Vivosmart 5 smartwatches to monitor metrics such as heart rate, HRV, and oxygen saturation, the attention-based model outperformed the baseline, achieving 95.40% testing accuracy and significantly reducing false positives and negatives. With precision, recall, and F1 scores of 0.982, this approach not only improves predictive performance but also offers interpretable results suitable for integration into IoT-enabled safety systems and BIM dashboards, advancing proactive, informatics-driven safety management in the construction industry.

</details>


### [62] [Detecting Data Contamination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19561)
*Juliusz Janicki,Savvas Chamezopoulos,Evangelos Kanoulas,Georgios Tsatsaronis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在黑盒假设下全面评估了现有最先进的成员推理攻击方法，并提出了一种新的Familiarity Ranking方法。实验结果表明，所有方法在多个LLM上的AUC-ROC均约为0.5，无法可靠地检测训练数据成员身份，揭示了在高级LLM上进行黑盒成员推理的固有困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在训练中可能使用受版权保护的数据，成员推理攻击（MIA）旨在检测特定文档是否被包含在模型的训练语料库中。然而，现有的黑盒MIA方法需要大量数据操作，且不同方法之间的比较面临挑战，缺乏在统一条件下对这些方法有效性的系统性评估。

Method: 研究者在黑盒假设下对最先进的成员推理攻击方法进行了系统性研究，使用统一的数据集对这些方法进行公平比较。此外，提出了一种名为Familiarity Ranking（熟悉度排序）的新方法，该方法允许LLM在表达上拥有更多自由度，从而更好地理解模型的推理过程。

Result: 实验结果显示，所有评估的MIA方法在多个大型语言模型上的AUC-ROC均约为0.5，表明没有任何方法能够可靠地检测训练数据的成员身份。对于更先进的LLM，更高的真阳性率（TPR）和假阳性率（FPR）反映了其更强的推理和泛化能力，这进一步凸显了黑盒MIA在检测LLM训练数据成员身份方面的困难。

Conclusion: 当前的黑盒成员推理攻击方法无法可靠地检测文档是否被用于训练大型语言模型。随着LLM推理和泛化能力的提升，成员检测变得更加困难。这表明现有的黑盒MIA方法在应对现代LLM时存在根本性局限，需要开发新的方法来应对这一挑战。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) utilize large amounts of data for their training, some of which may come from copyrighted sources. Membership Inference Attacks (MIA) aim to detect those documents and whether they have been included in the training corpora of the LLMs. The black-box MIAs require a significant amount of data manipulation; therefore, their comparison is often challenging. We study state-of-the-art (SOTA) MIAs under the black-box assumptions and compare them to each other using a unified set of datasets to determine if any of them can reliably detect membership under SOTA LLMs. In addition, a new method, called the Familiarity Ranking, was developed to showcase a possible approach to black-box MIAs, thereby giving LLMs more freedom in their expression to understand their reasoning better. The results indicate that none of the methods are capable of reliably detecting membership in LLMs, as shown by an AUC-ROC of approximately 0.5 for all methods across several LLMs. The higher TPR and FPR for more advanced LLMs indicate higher reasoning and generalizing capabilities, showcasing the difficulty of detecting membership in LLMs using black-box MIAs.

</details>


### [63] [Multi-modal Reasoning with LLMs for Visual Semantic Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19567)
*Chuou Xu,Liya Ji,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了视觉语义算术任务和IRPD数据集，并开发了SAri-RFT方法，通过强化学习后训练提升大型视觉语言模型从图像中推理语义关系的能力，在基准测试中取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习后训练方法主要关注大型语言模型在编码和数学推理方面的能力，但视觉语义算术能力（从图像中推理关系）尚未得到充分探索。从图像中提取简洁概念并进行关系推理比文本更具挑战性，因为它需要常识知识并从无关视觉细节中提取核心概念。这种能力对于非结构化环境中的服务机器人和家庭机器人至关重要，使其能够推断物体、智能体和动作之间的语义关系。

Method: 1. 形式化定义两个新任务：二项减法和三项运算；2. 构建Image-Relation-Pair Dataset (IRPD)基准数据集；3. 提出Semantic Arithmetic Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (SAri-RFT)方法，使用可验证函数和Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)对大型视觉语言模型进行后训练。

Result: 该方法在IRPD数据集和真实世界的Visual7W-Telling数据集上均达到了最先进的性能表现。实验证明了该方法在跨模态关系推理任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究通过强化学习后训练赋予大型视觉语言模型强大的跨模态关系推理能力，推动了家庭机器人在感知中建立符号推理基础的能力，增强了复杂环境下的决策制定、工具适应性和人机交互能力，为服务机器人在非结构化环境中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) as post-training is crucial for enhancing the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs) in coding and math. However, their capacity for visual semantic arithmetic, inferring relationships from images, remains underexplored. The classic text analogy "king"-"man"+"woman" = "queen" illustrates relational reasoning, yet replacing text with images of "king" and "man" significantly reduces performance because it requires commonsense knowledge and the extraction of concise concepts from irrelevant visual details. This capability is important for service and domestic robotics in unstructured environments, where robots must infer semantic relationships among objects, agents, and actions. In a kitchen, recognizing from images that "powder" and "cake" are related by "is made of" grounds symbolic relations in perception, enabling tool substitution, task generalization, and improved semantic reasoning. Prior work approaches semantic arithmetic by decoding image features after vector arithmetic, but suffers from modality gaps and lacks systematic evaluation. In this paper, we formulate two novel tasks, two-term subtraction and three-term operations, and construct the Image-Relation-Pair Dataset (IRPD) for benchmarking. We further propose Semantic Arithmetic Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (SAri-RFT), which post-trains large vision-language models (LVLMs) using a verifiable function and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Our method achieves state-of-the-art results on IRPD and the real-world Visual7W-Telling dataset. By equipping LVLMs with robust cross-modal relational reasoning, this work advances domestic robots' ability to ground symbolic reasoning in perception, enhancing decision-making, tool adaptability, and human-robot interaction in complex environments. Datasets and source code are provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [64] [Time Series Augmented Generation for Financial Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19633)
*Anton Kolonin,Alexey Glushchenko,Evgeny Bochkov,Abhishek Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个专门评估大语言模型（LLM）在金融时间序列分析任务中推理能力的新框架TSAG及相应基准，实验证明通过工具增强，SOTA模型能实现近乎完美的工具调用准确率且幻觉极少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准基准测试无法有效隔离和评估智能体（Agent）解析查询与编排计算的核心推理能力，因此在评估LLM处理复杂定量金融任务时存在关键且未解决的挑战。

Method: 提出了时间序列增强生成（TSAG）框架，让LLM智能体将定量任务委托给可验证的外部工具执行；构建了一个包含100个金融问题的基准，用于对比评估GPT-4o、Llama 3、Qwen2等多个SOTA智能体在工具选择准确性、忠实度及幻觉方面的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，在工具增强范式下，具备能力的智能体能够达到近乎完美的工具使用准确率，同时产生的幻觉极少，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究的主要贡献在于提出了该评估框架并通过大规模实证研究提供了关于智能体性能的洞察，已公开发布以促进可靠金融AI的标准化研究。

Abstract: Evaluating the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) for complex, quantitative financial tasks is a critical and unsolved challenge. Standard benchmarks often fail to isolate an agent's core ability to parse queries and orchestrate computations. To address this, we introduce a novel evaluation methodology and benchmark designed to rigorously measure an LLM agent's reasoning for financial time-series analysis. We apply this methodology in a large-scale empirical study using our framework, Time Series Augmented Generation (TSAG), where an LLM agent delegates quantitative tasks to verifiable, external tools. Our benchmark, consisting of 100 financial questions, is used to compare multiple SOTA agents (e.g., GPT-4o, Llama 3, Qwen2) on metrics assessing tool selection accuracy, faithfulness, and hallucination. The results demonstrate that capable agents can achieve near-perfect tool-use accuracy with minimal hallucination, validating the tool-augmented paradigm. Our primary contribution is this evaluation framework and the corresponding empirical insights into agent performance, which we release publicly to foster standardized research on reliable financial AI.

</details>


### [65] [SafetyALFRED: Evaluating Safety-Conscious Planning of Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19638)
*Josue Torres-Fonseca,Naihao Deng,Yinpei Dai,Shane Storks,Yichi Zhang,Rada Mihalcea,Casey Kennington,Joyce Chai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SafetyALFRED基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在具身环境中主动缓解安全风险的能力，发现现有模型虽能识别风险但难以有效应对，揭示了静态QA评估的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型正日益被用作交互环境中的自主智能体，但其主动应对安全隐患的能力尚显不足。现有的安全评估多通过脱离实体的问答（QA）设置关注危险识别，而缺乏对具身规划中主动风险缓解能力的评估。

Method: 在具身智能体基准ALFRED的基础上构建SafetyALFRED，增加了六类真实世界厨房危险场景。对来自Qwen、Gemma和 Gemini家族的11个最先进模型进行评估，不仅测试危险识别（QA设置），还测试通过具身规划进行主动风险缓解的能力。

Result: 实验结果揭示了一个显著的对齐差距：模型在QA设置中能准确识别危险，但这些危险的平均缓解成功率相对较低。

Conclusion: 静态QA评估不足以保障物理安全，研究倡导向优先考虑具身环境中纠正行动的基准转变。代码和数据集已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models are increasingly adopted as autonomous agents in interactive environments, yet their ability to proactively address safety hazards remains insufficient. We introduce SafetyALFRED, built upon the embodied agent benchmark ALFRED, augmented with six categories of real-world kitchen hazards. While existing safety evaluations focus on hazard recognition through disembodied question answering (QA) settings, we evaluate eleven state-of-the-art models from the Qwen, Gemma, and Gemini families on not only hazard recognition, but also active risk mitigation through embodied planning. Our experimental results reveal a significant alignment gap: while models can accurately recognize hazards in QA settings, average mitigation success rates for these hazards are low in comparison. Our findings demonstrate that static evaluations through QA are insufficient for physical safety, thus we advocate for a paradigm shift toward benchmarks that prioritize corrective actions in embodied contexts. We open-source our code and dataset under https://github.com/sled-group/SafetyALFRED.git

</details>


### [66] [A Dual Perspective on Synthetic Trajectory Generators: Utility Framework and Privacy Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19653)
*Aya Cherigui,Florent Guépin,Arnaud Legendre,Jean-François Couchot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对生成模型在人类移动数据隐私保护中的隐私-效用权衡问题，提出了一个新的效用评估框架，并设计了一种针对特定生成模型的成员推理攻击，证明了该类模型存在隐私漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动数据包含宗教信仰、政治派别等敏感信息，传统的聚合、混淆、噪声添加等隐私保护方法严重损害数据效用。新兴的生成模型方法在隐私-效用权衡方面的表现尚不明确，缺乏系统的评估框架和深入的隐私风险评估。

Method: 提出并应用了一个新的数据效用评估框架；依据欧盟现行法规，主张通过对抗性评估来应对隐私评估挑战；针对一类被认为具有隐私保护性（因其能抵抗轨迹用户关联问题）的生成模型子类，设计了一种新的成员推理攻击方法。

Result: 成功展示了新效用评估框架的应用；提供了证据表明隐私评估仍是一个巨大挑战；提出的成员推理攻击成功攻破了曾被认为具有隐私保护性的生成模型子类，揭示了其隐私漏洞。

Conclusion: 生成模型在人类移动数据隐私保护中的隐私-效用权衡问题仍待解决，隐私评估面临巨大挑战。应依据欧盟法规采用对抗性评估方法，且之前被认为安全的生成模型子类实际上存在隐私风险，亟需更完善的隐私保护机制。

Abstract: Human mobility data are used in numerous applications, ranging from public health to urban planning. Human mobility is inherently sensitive, as it can contain information such as religious beliefs and political affiliations. Historically, it has been proposed to modify the information using techniques such as aggregation, obfuscation, or noise addition, to adequately protect privacy and eliminate concerns. As these methods come at a great cost in utility, new methods leveraging development in generative models, were introduced. The extent to which such methods answer the privacy-utility trade-off remains an open problem. In this paper, we introduced a first step towards solving it, by the introduction and application of a new framework for utility evaluation. Furthermore, we provide evidence that privacy evaluation remains a great challenge to consider and that it should be tackled through adversarial evaluation in accordance with the current EU regulation. We propose a new membership inference attack against a subcategory of generative models, even though this subcategory was deemed private due to its resistance over the trajectory user-linking problem.

</details>


### [67] [A-MAR: Agent-based Multimodal Art Retrieval for Fine-Grained Artwork Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19689)
*Shuai Wang,Hongyi Zhu,Jia-Hong Huang,Yixian Shen,Chengxi Zeng,Stevan Rudinac,Monika Kackovic,Nachoem Wijnberg,Marcel Worring*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了A-MAR框架，通过基于结构化推理计划的条件检索来增强艺术品理解的解释性和证据基础，并构建了ArtCoT-QA基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在艺术品解释中依赖于隐式推理和内化知识，限制了其可解释性和明确的证据基础，难以满足艺术品理解所需的多步推理需求。

Method: 提出A-MAR（Agent-based Multimodal Art Retrieval）框架：首先将任务分解为结构化推理计划，明确每步的目标和证据需求；然后基于该计划进行条件检索，实现针对性证据选择；最终生成逐步的、有依据的解释。同时引入ArtCoT-QA基准用于评估多步推理能力。

Result: 在SemArt和Artpedia数据集上，A-MAR在最终解释质量上持续优于静态非计划检索和强MLLM基线；在ArtCoT-QA上的评估进一步证明了其在证据基础和多步推理能力方面的优势。

Conclusion: 推理条件检索对知识密集型多模态理解至关重要，A-MAR为构建可解释、目标驱动的AI系统迈出了一步，在文化产业等领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Understanding artworks requires multi-step reasoning over visual content and cultural, historical, and stylistic context. While recent multimodal large language models show promise in artwork explanation, they rely on implicit reasoning and internalized knowl- edge, limiting interpretability and explicit evidence grounding. We propose A-MAR, an Agent-based Multimodal Art Retrieval framework that explicitly conditions retrieval on structured reasoning plans. Given an artwork and a user query, A-MAR first decomposes the task into a structured reasoning plan that specifies the goals and evidence requirements for each step. Retrieval is then conditionedon this plan, enabling targeted evidence selection and supporting step-wise, grounded explanations. To evaluate agent-based multi- modal reasoning within the art domain, we introduce ArtCoT-QA. This diagnostic benchmark features multi-step reasoning chains for diverse art-related queries, enabling a granular analysis that extends beyond simple final answer accuracy. Experiments on SemArt and Artpedia show that A-MAR consistently outperforms static, non planned retrieval and strong MLLM baselines in final explanation quality, while evaluations on ArtCoT-QA further demonstrate its advantages in evidence grounding and multi-step reasoning ability. These results highlight the importance of reasoning-conditioned retrieval for knowledge-intensive multimodal understanding and position A-MAR as a step toward interpretable, goal-driven AI systems, with particular relevance to cultural industries. The code and data are available at: https://github.com/ShuaiWang97/A-MAR.

</details>

### [68] [Optimizing Branch Predictor for Graph Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18698)
*Upasna,Venkata Kalyan Tavva*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文指出图处理应用中分支预测错误频繁且严重影响性能，提出在优化缓存的同时，应进一步优化分支预测器以减少预测错误，从而提升整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的图应用通常超出缓存容量，内存层级已被认为是关键瓶颈。尽管优化缓存可以提升性能，但图处理中频繁发生的分支预测错误仍是主要性能限制，因此有必要通过提高分支预测准确性来进一步获得性能提升。

Method: 分析图处理应用中不同类型分支的静态与动态行为，评估现有分支预测器的不足，并提出对分支预测器进行针对性优化，以更好地处理导致预测错误的分支。

Result: 揭示了图处理应用中分支预测错误的频繁性及其对整体性能的重大影响，指出现有分支预测器仍有优化空间，能够更有效地处理引发预测错误的分支。

Conclusion: 在优化缓存之外，提升分支预测准确性是提升图处理应用性能的重要途径，未来的分支预测器设计应重点关注减少预测错误。

Abstract: Real-world graph applications are generally larger than the size of the cache itself. Due to this reason, the memory hierarchy was identified as a key bottleneck by the earlier works. Undoubtedly, the performance can be achieved by improving cache, there is still a scope for performance gain by improving branch prediction accuracy. In graph processing applications, the occurrence of branch mispredictions is very frequent and is a major limitation for the overall performance. Within a program, there are different kinds of branches that recur throughout its execution. Although lots of branch predictors (BP) have been developed earlier to capture the static and dynamic behavior of branches. Branch predictors can yet be further optimized to handle the branches that cause mispredictions.

</details>


### [69] [CHICO-Agent: An LLM Agent for the Cross-layer Optimization of 2.5D and 3D Chiplet-based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18764)
*Qihang Wu,Aman Arora,Vidya A. Chhabria*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出CHICO-Agent，一个基于大语言模型的优化框架，用于解决2.5D/3D芯粒系统设计的复杂性问题，相比模拟退火基线能找到更低成本的配置并提供可解释的设计审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型和AI工作负载的快速增长推动单片硅达到光罩和经济极限，加速了2.5D/3D芯粒系统的采用。然而，这些系统需要在计算栈的多个层级（应用、架构、芯片和封装）进行协同设计，导致设计复杂性显著增加。由此产生的设计空间具有高度组合性，需要在延迟、能耗、面积和成本之间进行权衡。

Method: 提出CHICO-Agent，一个面向2.5D/3D芯粒系统的LLM驱动优化框架。该框架维护一个持久化知识库来捕获参数-结果趋势，并通过管理-领域多智能体工作流协调探索过程。

Result: 与模拟退火基线相比，CHICO-Agent能够找到更低成本的配置方案，并为设计者提供可解释的审计轨迹。

Conclusion: CHICO-Agent框架有效解决了2.5D/3D芯粒系统设计的组合优化挑战，在降低设计成本的同时保持了结果的可解释性，为设计者提供了有价值的决策支持。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language models (LLMs) and AI workloads has pushed monolithic silicon to its reticle and economic limits, accelerating the adoption of 2.5D/3D chiplet systems. However, these systems increase design complexity by requiring co-design across multiple levels of the computing stack, including application, architecture, chip, and package. The resulting design space is highly combinatorial, with trade-offs among latency, energy, area, and cost. To address this challenge, we propose CHICO-Agent, an LLM-driven optimization framework for 2.5D/3D chiplet-based systems. CHICO-Agent maintains a persistent knowledge base to capture parameter-outcome trends and coordinates exploration through an admin-field multi-agent workflow. Compared with a simulated-annealing baseline, CHICO-Agent finds lower-cost configurations and provides an interpretable audit trail for designers.

</details>


### [70] [A Comparative Analysis of ARM and x86-64 Laptop-Class Processors: Architecture, Assembly-Level Performance, and Energy Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18896)
*Mustafa Mert Özyılmaz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究对比分析了Apple M3（ARM架构）与AMD Ryzen 7 3750H（x86-64架构）处理器。实验显示，Ryzen平台在分支密集型的斐波那契基准测试中速度显著更快，而矩阵乘法测试中两者无显著差异。但在能效方面，Apple平台表现突出，在两项测试中能效分别提升约5.82倍和6.38倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARM架构与x86-64架构处理器在指令集设计、内存层次结构、核心组织、系统集成及功耗管理机制等方面存在根本性差异。为了深入理解这些架构差异对实际性能和能耗的具体影响，有必要对代表性平台进行系统性的架构分析与实验对比。

Method: 研究采用架构分析与实验评测相结合的方法。架构层面对比了AArch64定长加载存储设计与x86-64变长、内存操作数丰富的设计差异，分析了寄存器组织、调用约定、异构核心、内存行为及低功耗机制。实验层面使用两个原生汇编基准测试（递归斐波那契与整数矩阵乘法），结合重复计时测量、处理器能耗测量以及跨平台微架构计数器测量进行综合分析。

Result: 在性能方面，Ryzen平台在分支密集的斐波那契测试中具有决定性速度优势，而矩阵乘法测试中两个平台无显著性能差异。在能效方面，Apple平台优势显著，斐波那契测试能耗降低约5.82倍，矩阵乘法测试能耗降低约6.38倍。

Conclusion: 研究结果反映了平台层面的综合特性，而非纯粹的指令集架构（ISA）单一效应。性能与能效差异源于指令集结构、具体实现方式、系统集成度及测量方法论等多重因素的共同作用，体现了现代处理器设计中架构与实现协同演进的特点。

Abstract: ARM-based and x86-64 laptop processors differ not only in instruction-set design, but also in memory hierarchy, core organization, system integration, and power-management mechanisms. This study presents a combined architectural and experimental comparison of an Apple M3 system and an AMD Ryzen 7 3750H system. The architectural analysis contrasts AArch64's fixed-width load-store design with the variable-length, memory-operand-rich x86-64 instruction model, and discusses how register organization, calling conventions, heterogeneous core organization, memory behavior, and low-power mechanisms shape observed performance and energy characteristics. The experimental part uses two native assembly benchmarks: a recursive Fibonacci workload and an integer matrix-multiplication workload. The analysis combines repeated timing measurements, processor-energy measurements, and cross-platform microarchitectural counter measurements from matched portable-C profiling runs. The Ryzen platform is decisively faster on the branch-heavy Fibonacci benchmark, while matrix multiplication shows no meaningful timing advantage for either platform in the present measurements. In contrast, the Apple platform is markedly more energy-efficient, reducing energy-to-solution by approximately 5.82$\times$ on Fibonacci and 6.38$\times$ on matrix multiplication. These results are interpreted as platform-level findings rather than as pure ISA-only effects, reflecting differences in implementation, system integration, and measurement methodology in addition to instruction-set structure.

</details>


### [71] [ChipLight: Cross-Layer Optimization of Chiplet Design with Optical Interconnects for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18909)
*Kangbo Bai,Zhantong Zhu,Yifan Ding,Tianyu Jia*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出ChipLight，一种针对Chiplet和光互连技术的大规模分布式LLM训练集群的跨层多目标设计与优化方法，有效解决了通信瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模分布式大语言模型（LLM）训练中，设备间的通信成为关键的性能瓶颈。Chiplet技术虽能提升节点内性能，光互连（OI）技术适合节点间网络，但两者的结合应用缺乏系统性的设计与优化方法。

Method: 提出了ChipLight方法，首先构建了融合Chiplet架构、训练并行策略和OI网络拓扑的架构模型；随后设计了结合黑盒与白盒方法的设计空间探索流程，实现了跨层协同优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，ChipLight显著提升了训练效率，并为未来训练集群的开发提供了有价值的设计见解。

Conclusion: Chiplet与光互连技术的结合是克服LLM训练通信瓶颈的有效途径，ChipLight证明了跨层多目标协同优化方法的有效性，为未来高性能训练集群的设计提供了指导。

Abstract: In large-scale distributed LLM training, communication between devices becomes the key performance bottleneck. Chiplet technology can integrate multiple dies into a package to scale-up node performance with higher bandwidth. Meanwhile, optical interconnect (OI) technology offers long-reach, high-bandwidth links, making it well suited for scale-out networks. The combination of these two technologies has the potential to overcome communication bottlenecks within and across packages. In this work, we present ChipLight, a cross-layer multi-objective design and optimization method for training clusters leveraging chiplet and OI. We first abstract an architecture model for such complex clusters, co-optimizing chiplet architecture, training parallel strategy, and OI network topology. Based on such models, we tailor the design space exploration flow by combining both black-box and white-box methodologies. Evaluated by our experimental results, ChipLight achieves significantly improved training efficiency and provides valuable design insights for the development of future training clusters.

</details>


### [72] [Design Rules for Extreme-Edge Scientific Computing on AI Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19106)
*Zhenghua Ma,G Abarajithan,Dimitrios Danopoulos,Olivia Weng,Francesco Restuccia,Ryan Kastner*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对极端边缘科学应用，系统对比了AI Engine与可编程逻辑（PL）在神经网络部署上的优劣，提出了LARE指标以指导硬件选择，并通过优化实现了在AI Engine上部署PL无法容纳的大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端边缘科学应用对延迟和吞吐量有严苛要求，传统的空间数据流在可编程逻辑上难以扩展至较大模型。现代FPGA SoC上的AI Engine提供了高计算密度和片上内存，但其架构与编程模型差异巨大，何时以及如何使用AI Engine替代可编程逻辑尚不明确。

Method: 作者对AI Engine和可编程逻辑进行了系统的架构特征分析与微基准测试；提出了延迟调整资源等价（LARE）指标，用于量化比较两种实现方案；针对低延迟科学推理，提出了空间级和API级的数据流优化方法。

Result: 所提出的LARE指标能有效识别AI Engine实现优于可编程逻辑设计的场景；通过优化，成功在AI Engine上部署了使用hlsml工具链时在可编程逻辑上无法容纳的端到端神经网络。

Conclusion: AI Engine在处理大规模极端边缘科学神经网络时具有显著优势，结合本文提出的LARE指标与优化策略，可以有效指导开发者选择合适的硬件实现方案，突破可编程逻辑的资源限制。

Abstract: Extreme-edge scientific applications use machine learning models to analyze sensor data and make real-time decisions. Their stringent latency and throughput requirements demand small batch sizes and require that model weights remain fully on-chip. Spatial dataflow implementations are common for extreme-edge applications. Spatial dataflow works well for small networks, but it fails to scale to larger models due to inherent resource scaling limitations. AI Engines on modern FPGA SoCs offer a promising alternative with high compute density and additional on-chip memory. However, the architecture, programming model, and performance-scaling behavior of AI Engines differ fundamentally from those of the programmable logic, making direct comparison non-trivial and the benefits of using AI Engines unclear. This work addresses how and when extreme-edge scientific neural networks should be implemented on AI Engines versus programmable logic. We provide systematic architectural characterization and micro-benchmarking and introduce a latency-adjusted resource equivalence (LARE) metric that identifies when AI Engine implementations outperform programmable logic designs. We further propose spatial and API-level dataflow optimizations tailored to low-latency scientific inference. Finally, we demonstrate the successful deployment of end-to-end neural networks on AI Engines that cannot fit on programmable logic when using the hlsml toolchain.

</details>


### [73] [Energy Efficient LSTM Accelerators for Embedded FPGAs through Parameterised Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19293)
*Chao Qian,Tianheng Ling,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对资源受限嵌入式FPGA优化的LSTM硬件加速器设计，实现了11.89 GOP/s/W的能效和32873样本/秒的实时推理速度，相比相关工作在执行速度和能耗方面均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LSTM网络是适用于嵌入式设备本地传感器数据流时间序列分析的重要深度学习技术，但资源受限的嵌入式FPGA平台对LSTM的硬件实现提出了特殊挑战。现有方案在执行速度和能耗方面仍有改进空间，需要专门优化的硬件加速器设计。

Method: 提出了专门针对资源受限嵌入式FPGA优化的LSTM硬件加速器设计。该设计包含多个可配置的优化参数（如DSP使用策略、激活函数实现方式等），使其能够适应不同的应用场景。研究详细阐述了关键设计决策并进行了性能评估。

Result: 加速器在实时推理中实现了11.89 GOP/s/W的能效，处理速度达到32873样本/秒。相比相关工作，在执行速度和能耗方面均有改善。该设计还具有良好的适应性，可通过优化参数调整以适应不同情况。

Conclusion: 所提出的LSTM硬件加速器设计成功解决了资源受限嵌入式FPGA平台的实现挑战，在能效和处理速度方面取得了优异性能，为嵌入式设备上的时间序列分析提供了有效的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Long Short-term Memory Networks (LSTMs) are a vital Deep Learning technique suitable for performing on-device time series analysis on local sensor data streams of embedded devices. In this paper, we propose a new hardware accelerator design for LSTMs specially optimised for resource-scarce embedded Field Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGAs). Our design improves the execution speed and reduces energy consumption compared to related work. Moreover, it can be adapted to different situations using a number of optimisation parameters, such as the usage of DSPs or the implementation of activation functions. We present our key design decisions and evaluate the performance. Our accelerator achieves an energy efficiency of 11.89 GOP/s/W during a real-time inference with 32873 samples/s.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [74] [Direction-Dependent Path Loss Modeling in Olive Orchards for Precision Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19427)
*Mohammad Rowhani Sistani,Katarzyna Kosek-Szott,Pierluigi Gallo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对行结构橄榄园中的无线传播特性，提出了一种基于拓扑的传播模型，相比传统模型能更准确地拟合实测RSSI数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园中的无线链路接收信号强度表现出显著的变异性，经典基于距离的传播模型无法充分描述。在行结构的橄榄园中，沿行与跨行方向的信号衰减差异明显，而自由空间路径损耗（FSPL）模型或ITU-R植被损耗公式等全向传播假设会导致预测偏差。

Method: 提出一种基于拓扑的传播模型，显式考虑果园布局及无线电设备在种植结构内的相对位置，从而区分沿行与跨行传播方向的影响。使用工作在868 MHz的LoRa技术进行实验验证，并将结果与文献中的经典模型及所提二维模型进行对比。

Result: 所提出的模型比传统模型更贴近实测RSSI数据，能够更准确地反映行结构橄榄园中的信号传播特性。

Conclusion: 该拓扑感知模型为结构化农业环境中的链路预算和网络规划提供了更可靠的依据，适用于类似的行栽作物种植园无线网络部署。

Abstract: Wireless links deployed in orchards often exhibit significant variability in the strength of the received signal that is not adequately captured by classical distance-based propagation models. In row-structured olive groves, signal attenuation differs markedly between along-row and cross-row propagation directions, leading to discrepancies when using omnidirectional propagation assumptions such as those adopted in the Free Space Path Loss (FSPL) model or ITU-R vegetation loss formulations. This paper proposes a topology-based propagation model that explicitly accounts for orchard layout and the relative positions of radio devices within the plantation structure. Experimental validation was conducted using LoRa technology operating at 868 MHz, and the results were compared with established models from the literature and with the proposed two-dimensional model. The proposed approach achieves a closer fit to measured RSSI data than conventional models, providing a more reliable basis for link budgeting and network planning in structured agricultural environments.

</details>


### [75] [Revisiting and Expanding the IPv6 Network Periphery: Global-Scale Measurement and Security Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19487)
*Zixuan Xie,Zitao Yang,Shurui Fang,Zhaoyang Li,Wenxing Xie,Nannan Fu,Liangyu Dong,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对IPv6网络边界安全进行了全球范围的全面评估，发现活跃边界数量大幅增长，服务暴露、路由环路等安全风险依然普遍存在，并提出了新的前缀选择策略和LLM暴露验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着IPv6部署加速，网络边界安全态势日益重要。DSN 2021的研究首次进行了大规模IPv6网络边界发现，但仅覆盖三个地区且数据已过时。需要更全面、最新的安全评估来理解当前IPv6网络边界的安全状况。

Method: 提出响应引导前缀选择(RGPS)策略以识别高价值IPv6前缀，支持高效大规模扫描；设计分层LLM暴露验证(HLEV)框架识别LLM部署工具中的未授权访问风险；对73个国家/地区进行全球规模测量。

Result: 识别出超过2.819亿活跃IPv6网络边界，相比2021年的印度、中国、美国数据增长371.2%；发现2.5%可达服务仍存在危险暴露；识别450万易发生路由环路的响应，证实缺陷路由行为在厂商和国家间仍广泛存在。

Conclusion: IPv6采用率激增，但关键安全挑战持续存在且具有结构性特征，包括服务暴露、路由环路漏洞以及LLM部署工具的安全弱点等问题。

Abstract: As IPv6 deployment accelerates, understanding the evolving security posture of network peripheries becomes increasingly important. A DSN 2021 study introduced the first large-scale discovery of IPv6 network peripheries, uncovering risks like service exposure and routing loops. However, its scope was limited to three regions and is now outdated. In this paper, we revisit and significantly expand upon that work, presenting a comprehensive, up-to-date security assessment of IPv6 network peripheries. To support efficient large-scale scanning, we propose a novel Response-Guided Prefix Selection (RGPS) strategy to identify high-value IPv6 prefixes for probing. Our global-scale measurement covers 73 countries/regions and identifies over 281.9M active IPv6 network peripheries, including a 371.2\% increase (245M) over the 52M reported in 2021 for India, China, and America. Our service exposure analysis shows that 2.5\% of reachable services are still dangerously exposed, including outdated administrative interfaces and misconfigured servers, while correlation with known CVEs reveals recurring software vulnerabilities. Building on this service-exposure perspective, we further design a Hierarchical LLM Exposure Verification (HLEV) framework to identify unauthorized-access risks in exposed LLM deployment tools, revealing multiple security weaknesses caused by insecure default configurations and missing authentication. Additionally, we revisit routing loop vulnerabilities and identify 4.5M loop-prone responses, confirming that flawed routing behaviors remain widespread across vendors and countries/regions. These findings suggest that while IPv6 adoption has surged, key security challenges persist and are structurally embedded.

</details>


### [76] [ZODIAC: Zero-shot Offline Diffusion for Inferring Multi-xApps Conflicts in Open Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19610)
*Zeyu Fang,Shu Hong,Huu Trung Thieu,Nakjung Choi,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了ZODIAC框架，首次在O-RAN中实现仅利用单个xApp的边缘数据即可进行零样本冲突条件推理，无需联合执行轨迹。该方法在所有三种O-RAN冲突类型上均显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN中多供应商xApp的并发执行可能引发开发阶段难以察觉的冲突，而现有冲突管理方法严重依赖实践中往往不可用的联合执行数据。因此需要一种能够仅基于各xApp独立边缘数据集进行冲突推理的方法。

Method: 提出ZODIAC三阶段框架：(1)不确定性感知代理模型训练；(2)轨迹级扩散训练；(3)组合引导去噪实现高效、物理约束且可靠的条件搜索。理论上推导出置信下界，证明组合推理可作为真实冲突严重度的原则性代理，其中认知惩罚直接控制近似差距。

Result: 在Mobile-Env平台（涵盖直接、间接、隐式三种O-RAN冲突类型）和 realistic NS-O-RAN-Flexric模拟器上评估，ZODIAC在Top-20真正率上比基线高出20%以上，Spearman等级相关性显著更强，场景多样性更高，且计算效率具有竞争力。消融研究证实各引导组件的必要性，认知不确定性惩罚对过滤虚假冲突至关重要。

Conclusion: ZODIAC是O-RAN领域首个无需联合执行轨迹即可从边缘离线数据进行冲突推理的框架，为解决xApp冲突管理中的数据稀缺问题提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) enables network control through multi-vendor xApps operating both within and across layers, subnets, and domains, whose concurrent execution can trigger conflicts that are latent during the development phase. Existing conflict management approaches rely heavily on joint-execution data, which is often unavailable in practice. To address this limitation, we formalize a novel problem termed conflict reasoning, which involves identifying conflict-inducing conditions given only marginal datasets from each individual xApp. We propose ZODIAC, a three-stage framework for zero-shot conflict condition inference that comprises uncertainty-aware surrogate model training, trajectory-level diffusion training, and compositional guided denoising for efficient, physics-constrained, and reliable condition search. We derive a theoretical lower confidence bound showing that the compositional reasoning in ZODIAC serves as a principled surrogate for true conflict severity, with the epistemic penalty directly controlling the approximation gap. We evaluate ZODIAC on both the lightweight Mobile-Env platform across all three O-RAN Alliance conflict types (direct, indirect, and implicit) and a realistic NS-O-RAN-Flexric simulator. ZODIAC consistently outperforms baseline condition search methods, achieving over 20% higher True Positive Rate at Top-20, substantially stronger Spearman rank correlation, greater scenario diversity, and competitive computational efficiency.
